# Fix status (2026-09-20, builds clean, NOT yet run in game)

**Applied** - crash: inventory-1, inventory-2, inventory-3, pause-texts-1 (= harmony-1), pause-texts-3, widgets-1, harmony-2, harmony-3 (= world-ui-1), harmony-5. Broken: crafting-1 (= inventory-4), crafting-2 (= auga-lib-2), crafting-3, crafting-4, crafting-6, auga-lib-3 (partly, via m_reqList), hud-bars-4, hud-build-5, hud-build-7, harmony-8 (partly), harmony-9, harmony-13, text-chat-1, text-chat-2, text-chat-5 (chat only), text-chat-7, minimap-1, pause-texts-2, pause-texts-5, pause-texts-6, pause-texts-9, pause-texts-13, auga-lib-5, widgets-2, widgets-5, widgets-10, world-ui-4, main-menu-1, inventory-6.

**Deliberately deferred**
- hud-build-1, hud-build-2, auga-lib-1: 1.0's build menu is the new `hudroot/BuildUIV2` (`BuildUi`); Auga's skin targets the old window that 1.0 never shows. Harmless dead code; a real fix is new prefab work.
- hud-bars-1, harmony-4: no Auga adrenaline bar. The vanilla bar is left visible on purpose - hiding it would remove a gameplay readout.
- main-menu-4 and other main-menu list prefabs: only matter once the Auga world/server list swap is re-enabled.
- minimap-3: touch-input only.
- widgets-6 (TabHandler.m_tabKeyInput defaults to true on Auga's copies), crafting-8, main-menu-3: need a look in the running game first.
- text-chat-4: Auga's raven text view was already a stub in 2023 (prefab work).
- Everything under "missing" and "cosmetic".
# Auga on Valheim 1.0 - static audit (2026-09-20)

16 agents (12 reviewers by UI area, 4 adversarial verifiers), read-only. 106 findings: confirmed=85, partly=20, refuted=0, cannot_verify=1.

## Area summaries

### inventory (7 findings)

The run-4 `InventoryGrid.UpdateGui` NullReferenceException is fully explained and is NOT the InventoryElement component itself: `PortInventoryElement` now succeeds (log: "InventoryElement added to slot prefab 'InventoryElement'"), but 1.0's element-creation loop reaches one line further and does `UIDragHandler componentInChildren2 = obj.GetComponentInChildren<UIDragHandler>(); componentInChildren2.m_onBeginDrag = ...` (decomp/InventoryGrid.cs:282-285). `UIDragHandler` is a class that only exists in 1.0's assembly_guiutils (absent from decomp_old_assembly_guiutils/), so Auga's 2023 slot prefab cannot carry it — I enumerated every component on InventoryElement.prefab (RectTransform, CanvasRenderer, Image, Button, UIInputHandler=943462877, UITooltip=438561839, AugaUnity ItemTooltip/FoodIndicator/QualityIndicator/BetterDurabilityBar/CooldownController, plus GuiBar on "durability"; fileIDs verified by recomputing Unity's MD4 script hash) and there is no UIDragHandler anywhere in the prefab. The throw happens on the first slot, before `m_elements.Add`, while `m_width/m_height` were already set — which is why the error appears exactly once and the grid then renders zero slots forever. After that fix the very next unconditional dereference in the same method, `element.m_canBeDroppedOn = CanDropDragOntoItem(item)` (decomp/InventoryGrid.cs:375), will throw, because Auga's `InventoryGui.Awake` postfix swaps `m_playerGrid`/`m_containerGrid` for fresh instances and re-attaches only `m_onSelected` and `m_onRightClick`, dropping the six other delegates vanilla wires at decomp/InventoryGui.cs:383-407. I walked every remaining dereference in 1.0's UpdateGui/UpdateGamepad/GetElement/GetHoveredElement/CreateItemTooltip against the prefab data and found no further crash: m_gridRoot ("Root"), m_uiGroup (PlayerPanel/ContainerPanel UIGroupHandler), the "binding" direct child with a TMP_Text, m_icon/m_amount/m_quality/m_equiped/m_queued/m_noteleport/m_food("foodicon")/m_selected/m_tooltip/m_durability(GuiBar with m_bar set) and the port's hidden "dropfocus" stand-in are all present, and a null `m_tooltipAnchor` is tolerated by `UITooltip.Set`. Cross-area notes (not counted below): 1.0 `HotkeyBar` still uses its own private ElementData with `transform.Find("binding"/"icon"/"durability"/...)`, so the HUD hotbar element prefab does NOT need the InventoryElement treatment; the crafting/right panel is the main victim of the duplicated `m_uiGroups[2]==m_uiGroups[3]` entry; and `root/SplitDialog` is untouched by Auga, so the new `SplitDialog` component and `InventoryGui.m_splitDialog` are intact and split-by-shift-click will work once the grid renders. I could not statically determine touch-mode behaviour or whether `UIGroupHandler.m_resetActiveElementOnStateChange` (new, initializer `= true`) deserializes as true or false on Auga's bundle copies, so I left that out.

### crafting (10 findings)

Auga's crafting half no longer crashes on 1.0: the previously fatal `m_uiGroups[3]` IndexOutOfRangeException (logs/auga_run3.log at `InventoryGui.OnTabCraftPressed` <- `AugaCraftingPanel.OnTabChanged` <- `WorkbenchPanelController.OnEnable`) is gone in run4 because PlayerInventory_Setup.cs now pads the array to four entries, and PortCarryOver keeps `root/Crafting` alive as a donor so every InventoryGui crafting field stays non-null (run4: "[PortDiagnostics] InventoryGui: 0 destroyed, 0 unassigned"). What remains is a set of silent behaviour losses, because Auga's crafting panel re-implements a 2023 UI and predates everything 1.0 added to the crafting loop: multi-craft (m_multiCraftAmount/m_multiCraftDuration/AltPlace + touch long-press), upgrader stations (CraftingStation.m_upgrader/m_hasCraftTab, Piece.Requirement.m_upgraderResource, upgrade-past-max), the skill-scaled craft duration (m_craftDurationSkillMaxDecrease) and the craft bonus. A grep of the whole Auga tree for any of those identifiers returns zero hits. I verified every claim against decomp/InventoryGui.cs, decomp/Player.cs, decomp/CraftingStation.cs, decomp/Piece.cs and decomp/Recipe.cs, and against dumps/InventoryGui_vanilla.txt for path strings (root/Crafting/RecipeList/Recipes/RecipeElement still has icon/name/Durability/QualityLevel/selected, all of which Auga's RecipeElement.prefab has, plus a Button - so AddRecipeToList and SetRecipe are safe). One thing I could NOT determine statically: the .prefab YAML under AugaUnity/Assets/Prefabs is out of sync with the shipped AssetBundles/augaassets - AugaCraftingPanel's TabController, WorkbenchName, DummyName, DummyDescription and all four CraftingRequirementsPanel instances' WorkbenchLevel/ItemCraftType read `{fileID: 0}` there, yet at runtime Initialize() dereferences TabController without throwing and PortDiagnostics reports zero unassigned InventoryGui references. I therefore based no finding on a null reference *value* in the prefab YAML (only on object names and component presence); a porter should re-check any prefab-side fix against the real bundle. Cross-area only: DoCrafting's new craft-bonus feedback runs through `DamageText.instance.ShowText(DamageText.TextType.Bonus, ...)` (DamageText is an Auga replacement), Auga's `InventoryGui.UpdateCharacterStats` prefix returns false and so drops 1.0's new `m_pvp.interactable = player.CanSwitchPVP()` / `player.SetPVP(m_pvp.isOn)`, and the run4 `InventoryGrid.UpdateGui` NRE aborts `InventoryGui.Update()` before `UpdateRecipe`/`UpdateRepair` ever run, so the logs can neither confirm nor deny anything downstream of it inside the crafting panel.

### hud-bars (5 findings)

The HUD-vitals area is exception-free on 1.0 — none of the four run logs contain a single Hud stack frame, and `[PortDiagnostics] Hud: 0 destroyed, 15 unassigned` accounts exactly for the 15 fields Auga deliberately nulls in `Hud_Awake_Postfix` (m_healthBar*/m_food*/m_staminaBar2*/m_eitr*), every one of which is only reachable from a method Auga prefixes with `return false`. All of Auga's vanilla path lookups under Hud (`hudroot/StatusEffects`, `hudroot/crosshair`, `hudroot/GuardianPower`, `hudroot/staggerpanel`, `hudroot/ShipHud`, `hudroot/action_progress`, `LoadingBlack`, `hudroot/SaveIcon`, `hudroot/BadConnectionIcon`, `hudroot/EventBar`, `hudroot/Damaged`, `hudroot/KeyHints`, `hudroot/HotKeyBar`) still resolve in the 1.0 dump, and every Auga-side path it then reads (`Dummy/HoverName`, `Sneak/Alert`, `PieceHealthRoot/PieceHealthBar`, `GPTimeText`, `staggerbar/RightBar/Background/FillMask/FillFast`, `Controls/RudderIndicatorBG/...`) exists in HUD.prefab/crosshair.prefab. `GuiBar`, `StatusEffect` (m_ttl/m_time/GetIconText/m_icon/m_flashIcon), `Player.Food`, `GetTotalFoodValue`, `GetStaggerPercentage`, `GetGuardianPowerHUD`, `Skills.GetSkillDef` and the Max/Get Health/Stamina/Eitr API are all byte-for-byte unchanged, so AugaHealthBar / AugaStaggerBar / AugaStatusEffects / AugaGuardianPowerHUD / PlayerPanelEffectController stay correct. `StatusEffect.m_hidden` (new) is already filtered inside `SEMan.GetHUDStatusEffects`, which is the only list Auga reads, so 1.0's new hidden-effect filtering is inherited for free. `m_lavaWarningScreen` is declared on Hud but referenced nowhere in any 1.0 assembly, so it is a non-issue. What does break is silent, not fatal: 1.0's brand-new adrenaline bar runs on an untouched vanilla branch, and two new Hud fields (`m_loadingIndicator`, `m_gpTouchButton`) resolve to objects that now live inside hidden `_VanillaDonor` branches. I could not determine statically the exact on-screen coordinates of `hudroot/adrenalinepanel` (the vanilla RectTransform anchors are not in any material I have), only that Auga never moves or restyles it. Cross-area notes: (a) 1.0 routes the whole build menu through the new `Hud.m_buildUi`/`BuildUi.Open/Close`, so `Hud.TogglePieceSelection`/`HidePieceSelection`/`IsPieceSelectionVisible` no longer touch `m_pieceSelectionWindow` at all — everything Auga restyles in `Hud_Awake_Postfix` lines 143-199 (Darken, Bkg2, category fonts, `m_pieceListRoot`, `m_pieceIconPrefab`) is attached to a window 1.0 never shows, and the un-restyled vanilla BuildUi appears instead; (b) in 1.0 the category tabs moved from `Categories/*` to `Categories/Tabs/*`, so Auga's `categories.GetChild(i)` loop (Hud_Setup.cs:164-191) matches no Button and silently restyles nothing; (c) `Hud.SetVisible` now dereferences `JoinCode.m_instance.m_root` and `Menu.instance.m_root` instead of the Menu transform, which belongs to the pause-menu reviewer.

### hud-build (9 findings)

Valheim 1.0 replaced the build menu wholesale: the legacy piece-selection window (`hudroot/BuildHud/bar/SelectionWindow`, still `Hud.m_pieceSelectionWindow`) is deactivated in `Hud.Awake` and never shown again, and a brand-new `hudroot/BuildUIV2` object carrying `BuildUi` + `TabHandler` (tabs By Usage / By Material / Recent / Favorites, search field, tag list, scrolling piece grid, favourites dropdown) is what `Hud.TogglePieceSelection` now opens. Auga's entire build-hud work targets the legacy window, so the single biggest effect in this area is not an exception but total loss of the feature: the player gets the unstyled vanilla 1.0 build menu, and Auga's `BuildHud.prefab`, `BuildHudElement.prefab`, `BuildMenuPaginationController` and `BuildHudTabFixer` are all inert (the prefab is instantiated under a GameObject that `Hud_Setup` deliberately deactivates first). The still-live parts of Auga's build UI are the piece-info panel patches (`SetupPieceInfo`, `UpdateBuild`), and those have real 1.0 gaps. I verified every path string Auga looks up against `dumps/Hud_vanilla.txt` (captured as a prefix to `Hud.Awake`, i.e. the raw 1.0 prefab): all `Replace()` paths in `Hud_Setup` resolve, so `Hud_Awake_Postfix` runs to completion — the run4 log confirms this (the KeyHints carry-over message at `Hud_Setup.cs:201` is printed). KeyHints is the one place with genuine NRE potential (`m_radialKeyHints`, `m_buildMenuHintsKB/GP` are dereferenced unguarded by 1.0 code and are empty on Auga's 2023 serialized copy); `PortCarryOver` fills all five from the donor (logged verbatim in run4), so no crash, but the objects it filled live in the hidden donor branch and are therefore invisible. HotkeyBar is clean: 1.0's new `GetComponent<Button>().onClick.AddListener` in `UpdateIcons` and `ToggleBindingHint`'s `Find("binding")` both resolve on Auga's `HotKeyElement.prefab` (it has a `Button`, guid 4e29b1a8…, plus binding/icon/durability/amount/equiped/queued/selected), and the field diff shows zero added/dropped HotkeyBar fields. What I could not determine statically: whether the 0.217.30 `Categories` object had the tab buttons as direct children (I only have the 1.0 hierarchy, where they sit one level deeper under `Categories/Tabs`), and the exact IL Roslyn emits for the new interpolated string in `UpdateBuild` (I reason C# 9 lowers it to `string.Format` with one combined literal). No build-mode exception appears in any of the four run logs because the tester never entered place mode. Cross-area notes only: `TabHandler` gained `m_cycling`/`m_tabKeyInput`/`m_keybaordInput`/`m_keyboardNavigateLeft`/`m_keyboardNavigateRight`/`m_blockingElements` — the build UI's TabHandler is vanilla and untouched, but Auga's five serialized TabHandler copies (Compendium/Inventory prefabs) will deserialize those new fields to type defaults rather than the C# initializers (`m_cycling = true`, `m_tabKeyInput = true`, `"TabLeft"`/`"TabRight"`), which `PortCarryOver` cannot fix because they are not Object references; and `Hud.m_gpTouchButton` (new in 1.0, the guardian-power touch/double-tap handler on `hudroot/GuardianPower/Icon`) is left pointing into the donor because `Hud_Setup.cs:82` replaces that branch after `Hud.Awake` already hooked it.

### minimap (5 findings)

The minimap survives 1.0 better than most Auga screens: every path string `Minimap_Setup.Minimap_Start_Postfix` looks up in Auga's own prefab still resolves (I rebuilt the `HUD.prefab` transform tree from YAML and cross-checked it against run4's `[PortDiagnostics] Minimap: 0 destroyed, 0 unassigned UI references`, which is runtime proof that m_nameInput, m_windMarker, m_gamepadCrosshair, m_publicPosition, m_sharedMapHint and all seven m_selectedIconN resolved). The port's `Fishlabs.GuiInputField` -> `GUIFramework.GuiInputField` rewrite is real and correct: Auga's `large/NameField` is a `TextField.prefab` instance carrying script fileID 1526933710 / guid a7c1e5d29b3f4c60a1d2f0e9b8c7d6a5, which I verified is MD4("s\0\0\0GUIFramework"+"GuiInputField"). The `Minimap.ShowPinNameInput` transpiler's IL pattern (`ldarg.0; ldc.i4.1; stfld Minimap::m_wasFocused`) still exists as the last statement of the 1.0 method body, so the patch still applies. PortCarryOver also removes the README's known `m_selectedIconPing` NRE, because the vanilla `large` branch now survives as the inactive `large_VanillaDonor` and the three new ping fields keep pointing into it. What is left is functional loss, not crashes: the whole 1.0 ping-pin UI and the whole 1.0 map key-hint block have no counterpart in Auga's 2023 prefab, the new touch open/close buttons are absent, and — the one that actually bites a mouse-and-keyboard player — map pins can no longer be named, because the only wire from the text field to `Minimap.OnPinTextEntered` runs through `UnityEngine.Input.GetKeyDown`, which is dead in 1.0. Run4 never opened the large map, so none of the large-map paths have live evidence; everything below is from the 1.0 decompile, the 1.0 hierarchy dump and Auga's prefab YAML. Cross-area notes (not counted as findings here): the same dead legacy `Input.GetKeyDown` sits in `AugaUnityLib/ChatWindowController.cs:23-24`, `AugaUnityLib/AugaCompendiumController.cs:153` and the unused `AugaUnityLib/InputFieldSubmitEvents.cs:23`, so chat close/scroll and the compendium Escape key are likely dead the same way; and `Minimap.Update` now dereferences `Hud.instance.m_buildUi.SearchFieldFocused` every frame, which run4 proves is non-null under Auga's Hud setup. I also confirmed Auga's `HUD.prefab` carries its own serialized `Minimap` component (fileID 2628571897053734972) with most references empty, but it is never instantiated — `Replace` only instantiates the `small`/`large` children — so it is inert.

### pause-texts (14 findings)

The pause-menu area has one hard, log-confirmed crash and a cluster of silent losses. The crash is a serialized TYPE change that PortCarryOver structurally cannot catch: 1.0 changed `Menu.m_skipButton` from `GameObject` to `Button` while keeping the name, Auga's AugaMenu.prefab still stores a `!u!1` GameObject there, so the field is non-null-but-invalid, carryover skips it, and `Menu.SetButtonsEnabled()` throws `NullReferenceException` at `Component.get_gameObject()` on the very first statement of every `Menu.Show()` (reproduced in auga_run1/2/4 logs). Because Show() aborts there, nothing after it runs either - no `Game.Pause()`, no `JoinCode.Show()`, no `UpdateNavigation()`, no `HandleInputLayoutChanged()`, and none of the other seven button visibility rules - so the pause menu opens unpaused and with a permanently visible SkipIntro entry that is miswired to `OnManualSave`. Beyond that, the run4 diagnostic line proves eleven Menu references were filled from the hidden donor; five of those (`m_gamepadRoot`, `m_gamepadMapController`, `m_cloudStorageWarning`, `m_cloudStorageWarningNextSave`, `m_inviteButton`) now point into an inactive GameObject, so the 1.0 gamepad button-map overlay, both cloud-storage dialogs and the new Invite Friends entry exist but can never be seen or clicked; `m_startScene` is a `SceneReference` struct that carryover cannot fill at all. I verified every path string against the live hierarchy dumps (`Menu_vanilla.txt`, `InventoryGui_vanilla.txt`), every field against decomp/decomp_old, and every prefab claim against the YAML. I could not determine statically: whether `Settings.Awake` throws before or after `m_tabKeyHints` (both paths are null-dereferences, but the first one reached decides the log text); whether Unity leaves `TabHandler`'s `= true` field initializers intact for bundle-deserialized components (I assume yes, which is what makes the Tab-key and m_activeTabEverSet findings hold); and the exact on-screen result of the compendium's un-run `SetActiveTab`, since the prefab's default page states happen to match tab 0. Cross-area: `PlayerInventory_Setup.cs:121` hides `root/Info` and never rebuilds it, which also removes the Skills/Texts/Trophies/PVP buttons (Auga re-implements those elsewhere, so only Achievements is actually lost); `AugaCompendiumController` lives on a child of the AugaMenu root, so `Menu_OnClose_Patch`'s `GetComponent<AugaCompendiumController>()` returns null and the compendium survives Hide() - that one is pre-existing, not a 1.0 regression.

### main-menu (7 findings)

The main-menu area is, at runtime, almost entirely vanilla: `Auga/MainMenu_Setup.cs` has its whole menu-replacement body inside a `/* */` block (lines 37-195 plus the four `FejdStartup` patches at 220-278), and `git show HEAD:Auga/MainMenu_Setup.cs` proves that block was already commented out upstream before the 1.0 port — the port's only change to that file was commenting out `m_settingsPrefab = Auga.Assets.SettingsPrefab` (documented in PORT_1.0.md). The only live Auga code in my area is `FejdStartup_Awake_Patch.Prefix` (a redundant `ZInput.Initialize()`; harmless because `SceneLoader.Awake` at decomp/SceneLoader.cs:71 already initialized it and 1.0's guard is `if (m_instance == null)`) and `Connection_Setup.ZNet_Awake_Patch`, which swaps `ZNet.m_passwordDialog`/`m_connectingDialog` for AugaPassword/AugaConnecting. I verified that swap end to end and it should work: PORT_1.0.md's rewrite of the 7 `Fishlabs.GuiInputField` refs (fileID -560918846) to `GUIFramework.GuiInputField` (fileID 1526933710) is correct — I recomputed both MD4 fileIDs and decompiled both assemblies; the two classes have identical serialized fields, no `-560918846` refs remain in `AugaUnity/Assets`, the field is masked (`m_ContentType: 7`, `m_InputType: 2`), it is active so `GetComponentInChildren<GuiInputField>()` at decomp/ZNet.cs:915 resolves, and run4 reaches "Loading: ZNet Start" with no exception. What I could not determine statically: there is no `ZNet_vanilla.txt` hierarchy dump, so I cannot see what the untouched 1.0 PasswordDialog/ConnectingDialog contain (buttons, UIGroupHandler, in-scene UnityEvent wiring) or whether the two dialogs share a parent — that is the one real blind spot, and findings 1 and 3 are about it. I also could not verify `portal_wood`'s `_target_found_red`/`New` children used by `AugaTeleportScreen.OnEnable` (live, on HUD.prefab's inactive `Teleporting` object), though I did confirm `GameCamera.m_camera`/`m_instance`, `DepthOfField`, `SunShafts` and `PostProcessingBehaviour` all still exist in 1.0. Cross-area (not mine, but it lands the player back in my screen): run4 shows `Menu` on `AugaMenu(Clone)` filled 11 refs from the donor but NOT `m_startScene`, which 1.0 added as a `SceneReference` struct — `PortCarryOver.FillComponent` only handles `UnityEngine.Object` fields and `List<Object>`, so `Menu.OnLogout`'s `SystemResourceManager.FastLoadScene(m_startScene)` (decomp/Menu.cs:621) will have an empty scene reference, and `PortDiagnostics` cannot see it either since it only scans Object fields.

### text-chat (10 findings)

Covered: Chat/Terminal (incl. the 1.0 GuiInputField move from ui_lib/Fishlabs to gui_framework/GUIFramework), TextInput and its TextReceiver clients (Sign, Tameable, TeleportWorld), TextViewer (Rune/Intro/Raven), Tutorial, Raven, MessageHud, DamageText, plus Auga's Chat_Setup/TextInput_Setup/TextViewer_Setup/MessageHud_Setup/DamageText_Setup/Text_Patch/AugaLog_Hooks and the AugaChat, AugaTextInput, AugaTextViewer, AugaMessageHud prefabs (prefab YAML parsed, animator controllers and clips read). Overall this area is in better shape than the brief implies: the porter already rewrote the 7 `Fishlabs.GuiInputField` script refs to `GUIFramework.GuiInputField` (guid a7c1e5d29b3f4c60a1d2f0e9b8c7d6a5, fileID 1526933710) in AugaChat/AugaTextInput/TextField, all TextViewer/MessageHud/DamageText/TextInput serialized fields still exist in 1.0 (auga_field_diff.csv shows 0 dropped, 0 new for those four), `Terminal.updateSearch` null-guards the unassigned `m_search` the logs report, and `Chat.NpcText.m_topicField` is assigned but never read so Auga's NpcDialog/Topic having a legacy UI.Text instead of a TextMeshProUGUI is harmless. No exception in any of the four runs originates in this area. INTRO: I could not find a static cause inside the text surfaces. auga_run1.log proves the path executed - "Show intro 47672" then "Show text YOU DIED:Long ago, the Allfather Oden..." - and the Valkyrie was held for 69 s ("Setting up valkyrie" 17:34:44 -> "Starting descent" 17:35:53), which only happens while `TextViewer.IsShowingIntro()` is true, i.e. Auga's IntroText.controller really did sit in `textview_show` (m_Tag: visible) for the whole 45.13 s `textview_introshown` clip, with CanvasGroup alpha 1 and the Crawl scrolling 0 -> 2043. So the TextViewer swap, the field bindings, the animator and the clip are all working; whatever hides it is outside this area - most likely draw order / an opaque Auga branch under `IngameGui` covering `AugaTextViewer(Clone)`, or Auga's replaced loading screen. HUGIN: Auga's only contact with the raven is the `Chat.SetNpcText` postfix, and I found no static path in Chat/Raven that suppresses the NPC dialog. Two cross-area suspects worth handing to the Hud reviewer: (a) 1.0's `Hud.UpdateCrosshair` (decomp/Hud.cs:829-831) gained `if (hoverable != null && !TextViewer.instance.IsVisible())` and dereferences `TextViewer.instance` unguarded - Auga patches `Hud.UpdateCrosshair` (Hud_Setup.cs:463) and re-routes `m_hoverName` into `AugaHoverText`, so if that re-implementation drops the text the "E Talk" prompt disappears and Hugin looks dead even though Chat works; (b) `decomp/CinematicsHider.cs` is a brand-new 1.0 class (absent from decomp_old_assembly_valheim) whose `Start()` registers its GameObject in `CinematicsManager.m_hiders` - Auga's replacement prefabs carry no `CinematicsHider` and `PortCarryOver` can only fill fields, never add components, while every donor is deactivated inside Awake so its `Start()` never runs, meaning every replaced UI branch silently drops out of the cinematic hide/restore list.

### world-ui (4 findings)

The in-world/small-screen area is in better shape than the big screens: the 1.0 field-level diff for EnemyHud (8/8), StoreGui (14/14) and PlayerCustomizaton (21/21) shows zero added or dropped serialized fields, so none of the classic "new field is null on Auga's 2023 copy" crashes apply here, and every child path 1.0 looks up (Health/health_fast, Health/health_slow, Name, level_2, level_3, Alerted, Aware for EnemyHud; icon, name, price, selected for the store element) exists in AugaEnemyHud.prefab / AugaStoreScreen.prefab. I verified the element-centring 1.0 added to StoreGui.FillList (num2 = (m_listRoot.rect.width - element.rect.width)/2) evaluates to exactly 0 for Auga (both rects are 230 wide), so it is harmless; that StoreGui is still on a GameObject named "Store_Screen" with a root panel named "Store", so Store_Setup's guard still matches; and that hudroot/MountHud and hudroot/ValheimRadial are untouched by Auga, so the 1.0 mount panel and the radial menu itself survive. Auga has no reference at all to Tameable, ItemStand or ArmorStand, and the 1.0 radial classes (OpenRadialConfig, RadialBase, ItemGroupConfig, RadialConfigHelper) have no coupling to any UI object Auga replaces. The real damage is: (1) Store_Setup's FillList postfix has not been taught about 1.0's prefab-less "Player Key Item" trade items, (2) StoreGui is the one screen that never goes through SetupHelper.DirectObjectReplace/PortCarryOver, so it gets no donor at all, and (3) the radial menu's 1.0 key-hint strip is stranded in the hidden KeyHints donor. What I could not determine statically: whether 1.0's shipped traders actually stock a prefab-less key item today (the Trader prefabs live in LZ4-compressed StreamingAssets bundles I could not decompress; the four separate m_prefab null-guards 1.0 added are my evidence), and the vanilla BarberGui's m_hairToolTier value (Auga's prefab has 0 and PortCarryOver only fills Object references, never ints, so a non-zero vanilla tier would silently hide tier-gated hair/beards). Cross-area notes: Auga's HudBase has no Health/health_fast_friendly child so 1.0's friendly-vs-enemy health-bar colouring on EnemyHud is lost (pre-existing, cosmetic); UIGamePad's new m_availableForConsoleMouse bool defaults to false on all 61 Auga UIGamePad instances, making IsBlocked() true whenever ZInput.IsGamepadMouseActive() (gamepad reviewer); and PlayerController.TakeInput now dereferences Hud.instance.m_buildUi.SearchFieldFocused every frame, which belongs to whoever owns hudroot/BuildUIV2.

### widgets (10 findings)

Shared widgets are in better shape than the screen-level replacements: GuiBar, UIInputHandler, Localize, ScrollRectEnsureVisible and Utils.FindChild/ClampUIToScreen are functionally unchanged in 1.0, so Auga's GuiBar_Patch (SetBar -> fillAmount), Text_Patch (UnityEngine.UI.Text.text setter, still a virtual property) and its 36 UITooltip / 28 GuiBar / 12 ScrollRectEnsureVisible prefab components still bind correctly, and every ZInput button name Auga hardcodes ("JoyMenu", "JoyButtonA/B", "JoyLStick*", "JoyTabLeft/Right") still exists. Both Auga tooltip prefabs are structurally compatible with vanilla's assumptions: SimpleTooltip and InventoryTooltip each have exactly one child at index 0 (so `m_tooltip.transform.GetChild(0)` for ClampUIToScreen is safe) and their "Text"/"Topic" children carry TextMeshProUGUI (guid f4688fdb7df04437aeb418b961361dc5), so `UpdateTextElements`'s unguarded `GetComponent<TMP_Text>()` will not NRE. The real damage is concentrated in three places: (1) ZInput's rewrite onto InputAction/InputSystem, where `GetBoundKeyString` now indexes `s_gamepadSpriteMap` and throws KeyNotFoundException for the always-registered `JoyRStickLeft`/`JoyRStickRight` defs - Auga enumerates every button def, so this is a hard, repeatable crash; (2) `UITooltip.OnHoverStart` no longer destroying the previous tooltip, which only matters because Auga (unlike vanilla) ships two different `m_tooltipPrefab`s; (3) PortCarryOver's scope - it only copies UnityEngine.Object references from components on the donor ROOT, so none of Auga's 36 UITooltip / 61 UIGamePad / 51 ButtonSfx / 32 UIGroupHandler / 7 UIInputHint children get any carryover, and every new value-type field 1.0 added to those classes silently takes its C# initializer default. Statically undeterminable: whether legacy `UnityEngine.Input` throws or merely returns false on this build (no InvalidOperationException appears in runs 1-4, but the project memory records BepInEx hotkeys silently failing, which is the fallback BepInEx takes only after catching that exception) - one live check of the Auga chat settles it; and whether Unity's AssetBundle type-tree transfer really leaves absent fields at their constructor values for these bundles, which finding 6 assumes. Cross-area notes for other reviewers: `KeyBindDisplay.ZInputId: GPower` in HealthBar.prefab (x5) and HUD.prefab refers to a button def that has never existed (vanilla's is "GP"), so those labels render `MISSING BUTTON DEF "GPower"` - pre-existing, not a 1.0 regression, but now user-visible next to the guardian-power HUD; `InventoryGrid.m_tooltipAnchor` (decomp\InventoryGrid.cs:411) and `StoreGui.m_tooltipAnchor` (logged as still unassigned in run4) feed the same UITooltip anchor path as finding 10; and PortCarryOver's decision to keep donors as hidden siblings means `Localization.Localize/ReLocalizeAll/ReLocalizeVisible` (which use `GetComponentsInChildren<TMP_Text>(true)`) now permanently register every donor's text in `textStrings`/`textMeshStrings`, a leak/perf cost rather than a correctness bug.

### auga-lib (10 findings)

AugaUnityLib (68 MonoBehaviours in the bundle) is in better shape than the plugin layer: the big 1.0 renames were already handled (PlayerProfile.m_playerStats is now indexed [0], ZInput.ButtonDef.ButtonAction replaces the old KeyCode field, Fishlabs.GuiInputField refs were rewritten to GUIFramework.GuiInputField, AttachItem/CountItems/GetWeight/GetTooltip only gained defaulted params). I checked every game-API call site in the 68 files against the 1.0 decompile (Player, Character/Humanoid, Skills, SEMan, StatusEffect, ItemDrop.ItemData, Inventory, InventoryGui, Hud, Game, ZInput, Localization, PlayerProfile, FejdStartup, PlayerCustomizaton, VisEquipment, ZDO, PieceTable, Recipe, Piece.Requirement, TabHandler, TextsDialog, SaveSystem, Utils, GameCamera, SE_Stats, HitData) plus the ZInput/Localization key-string path and the crafting/build-menu index maths. What is left is the silent class of breakage: nothing here throws, but several features now compute against 1.0 semantics that moved underneath them. The four live problem areas are (1) direct UnityEngine.Input reads — 1.0 removed every legacy Input call from game code and routes everything through ZInput's Input-System shim, so Auga's remaining Input.GetKeyDown sites are dead; (2) the build-menu pagination controller, which still equates a category-tab array index with a Piece.PieceCategory value after 1.0 renumbered the enum and made tabs index pieceTable.m_categories; (3) the crafting panel, which re-derives progress and requirement state with the 0.217 formulas and misses m_multiCraftDuration/m_upgraderDuration/crafting-skill scaling and m_upgraderResource/m_requireOnlyOneIngredient filtering; (4) ComplexTooltip, which re-implements the whole item tooltip and therefore drops everything 1.0 added to it (11 equipment modifiers, non-player damage, subtitle, cheated marker). I could not statically verify the AugaTeleportScreen portal-child path strings ("_target_found_red", "New" under portal_wood) or whether the main camera still carries SunShafts/DepthOfField in 1.0 — those need the game's asset bundle, not the decompile. Cross-area (not mine, but they land on lib components): 1.0 replaced Hud's build window with a new `BuildUi`/`BuildUIV2` branch and 1.0's Hud.UpdateBuild gates on `m_buildUi.gameObject.activeSelf`, while Hud_Setup.cs:157 still replaces children of `m_pieceSelectionWindow`; vanilla 1.0 now has 6 piece-category tabs (Misc, Crafting, Building, Building2, Furniture, Deep North) against Auga's smaller tab set; and the brand-new adrenaline bar fields on Hud (m_adrenalineBarRoot/Animator/BarFast/BarSlow/Text) have no Auga prefab at all.

### harmony (15 findings)

Auga applies ~55 Harmony patches (plus 2 reflective compat blocks). I checked every one against the 1.0.15 decompile: all targets still exist and every patch's `__result`/ref/static-vs-instance shape and every named parameter (`player`, `piece`, `dt`, `c`, `type`, `text`, `mySelf`, `amount`, `icon`, `quality`, `allowedQuality`, `item`, `tooltip`, `station`, `biome`, `skill`, `listRoot`, `scrollRect`, `i`) still resolves, so no patch fails to bind. The porter already fixed the two signature traps that mattered (`DamageText.AddInworldText` float→string, `Player.AddKnownBiome`/`IsBiomeKnown` Heightmap.Biome→BiomeSector); `InventoryGui.SetupRequirement`, `SetupRequirementList`, `Player.HaveRequirements` and `Game.SpawnPlayer` all gained optional/extra parameters that do not break by-name patching. None of the targets became an iterator, async or local function, and none moved class. The real damage is in the prefix-false / transpiler re-implementations and in fields whose *type* (not nullness) changed, which `PortCarryOver` by construction cannot repair: `Menu.m_skipButton` went GameObject→Button, so Auga's 2023 prefab holds a live-but-wrong-typed reference that Fill skips, and `Menu.SetButtonsEnabled` (new in 1.0, called from `Menu.Show`) throws on it — that is the `Menu.SetButtonsEnabled` NRE in auga_run4.log, i.e. the pause menu cannot be opened. Separately, the still-throwing `InventoryGrid.UpdateGui` NRE in run4 is almost certainly the new `UIDragHandler` component (new class in assembly_guiutils 1.0) that `PortInventoryElement` does not add to the slot prefab. Cross-area (mentioned, not filed): 1.0 moved the whole build menu to a new `hudroot/BuildUIV2` `BuildUi` component and never re-activates `m_pieceSelectionWindow`, so all of Auga's build-HUD prefab work is applied to a permanently hidden branch; and Auga's `Menu`/`InventoryGui`/`Hud` prefabs need rebuilding against the 1.0 field layout rather than being patched at runtime. I could not statically verify the sibling names `TopLeftMessage` and `Chat_box` used by `IndirectTwoObjectReplace` (the hierarchy dumps only contain each component's own subtree, not its siblings), nor whether any shipped 1.0 trader actually sells a prefab-less key item.

## Severity: crash

### [pause-texts-1] Menu.m_skipButton changed GameObject -> Button in 1.0; Auga's prefab still stores a GameObject, so SetButtonsEnabled NREs on every pause-menu open

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 97)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\AugaMenu.prefab:450 (Menu component &5026525812570277222)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Menu.SetButtonsEnabled() / Menu.Show() (decomp\Menu.cs:250, :228)
- **Trigger:** Press Escape (or gamepad Menu) in-game - every single time the pause menu is opened.
- **Consequence:** NullReferenceException at UnityEngine.Component.get_gameObject() -> Menu.SetButtonsEnabled -> Menu.Show -> Menu.Update. Show() aborts at its first SetButtonsEnabled statement, so Game.Pause(), JoinCode.Show(), UpdateNavigation(), m_rebuildLayout=true, ZInput.WorkaroundEnabled=false and HandleInputLayoutChanged() all never run: the menu appears but the game is NOT paused and no button-visibility rule is applied.
- **Evidence:** decomp_old_assembly_valheim\Menu.cs:65 `public GameObject m_skipButton;` vs decomp\Menu.cs:37 `public Button m_skipButton;` (name kept, type changed, no [FormerlySerializedAs] needed/possible). AugaMenu.prefab:450 `m_skipButton: {fileID: 4824936238869214461}` and AugaMenu.prefab:3217 `--- !u!1 &4824936238869214461 stripped / GameObject` - class id !u!1 is GameObject, not a component. SetButtonsEnabled's first line is `m_skipButton.gameObject.SetActive(...)`. Logs: auga_run4.log:356-361, auga_run2.log:402-407, auga_run1.log:478-483,497-502,519-524 all show `UnityEngine.Component.get_gameObject () / Menu.SetButtonsEnabled () / Menu.Show () / Menu.Update ()`. The field is absent from auga_run4.log:305's carryover list (`CurrentPlayersPrefab, m_continueButton, m_inviteButton, m_settingsButton, m_logoutButton, m_quitButton, m_cloudStorageWarning, m_cloudStorageWarningNextSave, m_feedbackPrefab, m_gamepadRoot, m_gamepadMapController`) because PortCarryOver.cs:106 tests `(Object)targetValue == null`, which is false for a live-but-wrong-typed PPtr.
- **Fix:** In AugaMenu.prefab re-point `m_skipButton` at the Button component on the SkipIntro MenuButton instance (a `!u!114` fileID under PrefabInstance &7881465935064588165), not at the GameObject. Until the bundle can be rebuilt, add a runtime fixup in Menu_Start_Patch.Postfix / PortCarryOver: for each UnityEngine.Object field whose value is non-null but not assignable to the field type (or whose `.gameObject` access fails), clear it and refill from the donor - or explicitly do `newMenu.m_skipButton = newMenu.transform.Find("MenuRoot/Menu/MenuEntries/SkipIntro").GetComponent<Button>()`.

### [inventory-1] Auga's slot prefab has no UIDragHandler; 1.0 InventoryGrid.UpdateGui dereferences it unconditionally (this IS the run4 NRE)

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 95)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\InventoryElement.prefab — root GameObject 'InventoryElement' (fileID 1830661766780744735), component list at lines 222-233; and C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\PortInventoryElement.cs:29-50 (Setup does not add it)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** InventoryGrid.UpdateGui — scratchpad\decomp\InventoryGrid.cs:282-285
- **Trigger:** Open the inventory (Tab) or any chest for the first time; runs once per grid when m_width/m_height first differ from the 4x4 defaults.
- **Consequence:** NullReferenceException inside InventoryGrid.UpdateGui on the very first slot (j=0,i=0), before m_elements.Add. m_width/m_height were already assigned, so the creation block never runs again: m_elements stays empty forever, the player and container grids render ZERO slots, GetHoveredElement()/GetElement() always return null and no tooltips appear. Exactly matches auga_run4.log lines 337-342, which is logged once and never repeats. With a gamepad there is a second NRE: InventoryGrid.GetGamepadSelectedElement (decomp/InventoryGrid.cs:643-647) passes the bounds check against m_width/m_height but GetElement() returns null out of the empty list, so `.transform` throws on every d-pad press and in InventoryGui.GetSelectedGamepadElement/GetSelectedSize.
- **Evidence:** 1.0: `UIDragHandler componentInChildren2 = obj.GetComponentInChildren<UIDragHandler>(); componentInChildren2.m_onBeginDrag = (Action<UIDragHandler>)Delegate.Combine(...)`. The old body Auga was built against has no such lines (see auga_audit\diffs\InventoryGrid.diff, the three `+` lines after `+ componentInChildren.m_onPointerEnter = ...`). `UIDragHandler.cs` exists in scratchpad\decomp_assembly_guiutils\ but NOT in scratchpad\decomp_old_assembly_guiutils\ — it is new in 1.0. Every MonoBehaviour on the Auga slot root, by m_Script fileID (guid d920e4723ba2566674efb2f0c3eb6edb = assembly_guiutils, 045bc81af5d75cb42a79645f3b1c6adb = Unity.Auga.dll): 943462877=UIInputHandler, 438561839=UITooltip, 1049120956=AugaUnity.ItemTooltip, 907649998=AugaUnity.FoodIndicator, 1319506116=AugaUnity.QualityIndicator, 777601199=AugaUnity.BetterDurabilityBar, -151086124=AugaUnity.CooldownController, plus UnityEngine.UI Image (fe87c0e1cc204ed48ad3b37840f39efc) and Button (4e29b1a8efbd4b44bb3f3716e73f07ff). No 1.0 UIDragHandler fileID appears anywhere in the file. All objects in the prefab are m_IsActive: 1, so the earlier `obj.GetComponentInChildren<UIInputHandler>()` does resolve and is not the throw site. Run 3 (auga_run3.log:337-340) threw in the same method with no '[PortDiagnostics] slot prefab' lines at all, i.e. before PortInventoryElement existed; run 4 added the component and moved the crash exactly one statement later.
- **Fix:** In PortInventoryElement.Setup, next to `element.m_button = prefab.GetComponent<Button>() ?? prefab.AddComponent<Button>();`, also ensure the drag handler exists on the slot root: `if (prefab.GetComponentInChildren<UIDragHandler>(true) == null) prefab.AddComponent<UIDragHandler>();`. It must be added before the first UpdateGui runs (the existing Priority.First prefix is the right place). Vanilla's own slot carries UIDragHandler beside UIInputHandler (see root/dropButton in auga_audit\dumps\InventoryGui_vanilla.txt line 3: [Button, Image, UIInputHandler, UIDragHandler]), so this is vanilla-equivalent and its IDragHandler will not regress anything the vanilla layout does not already do.

### [main-menu-4] WorldListElement.prefab has no 'modifiers' child and uses legacy UI.Text, so 1.0 UpdateWorldList NREs the moment the menu swap is re-enabled

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 95)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\MainMenu\WorldListElement.prefab; consumed by the commented-out `__instance.m_worldListElement = Auga.Assets.WorldListElement;` at Auga\MainMenu_Setup.cs:141
- **Vanilla 1.0:** FejdStartup.UpdateWorldList (decomp/FejdStartup.cs:1308, crash line 1338)
- **Trigger:** Opening Start Game -> world list, once MainMenu_Setup's block is uncommented.
- **Consequence:** NullReferenceException in FejdStartup.UpdateWorldList: `gameObject.transform.Find("modifiers")` returns null and is dereferenced without a guard. Even past that, `Find("seed").GetComponent<TMP_Text>()` and `Find("name").GetComponent<TMP_Text>()` return null because those children carry legacy UnityEngine.UI.Text, so `component2.text = ...` throws. The world list is unusable.
- **Evidence:** WorldListElement.prefab's complete child set is Button, selected, name, seed, source_local, source_legacy, source_cloud - there is no `modifiers`. `name` and `seed` each carry m_Script guid 5f7201a12d95ffc409449d95f23cf332, which Library/PackageCache/com.unity.ugui@7537063fea2b/Runtime/UGUI/UI/Core/Text.cs.meta identifies as UnityEngine.UI.Text; the prefab contains zero TextMeshProUGUI refs (guid f4688fdb7df04437aeb418b961361dc5). 1.0 line 1338: `gameObject.transform.Find("modifiers").GetComponent<TMP_Text>().text = Localization.instance.Localize(ServerOptionsGUI.GetWorldModifierSummary(...))`. Note this is pre-existing debt, not a 1.0 regression: 0.217.30's UpdateWorldList made the identical three Find() calls with TMP_Text (decomp_old_assembly_valheim/FejdStartup.cs), which is why the assignment is commented out.
- **Fix:** Rebuild WorldListElement.prefab in Unity: add a `modifiers` child and convert `name` and `seed` to TextMeshProUGUI (the source_* children are fine - 1.0 uses `?.` on those). Do not uncomment MainMenu_Setup.cs:141 before that.
- **Verifier's better fix:** Fix is right. Ordering nit for whoever debugs it: the first throw is at decomp/FejdStartup.cs:1337 (`component2.text = world.m_seedName` on the null TMP_Text from `seed`), not the `modifiers` NRE at :1338 — so converting seed/name to TextMeshProUGUI is the first blocker, adding the `modifiers` child the second.

### [harmony-5] The 'resetbiomes' console command writes a HashSet<Heightmap.Biome> into Player.m_knownBiome, which 1.0 retyped to HashSet<string>

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 93)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\Auga.cs:667-679 (Terminal_InitTerminal_Patch.Postfix)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Player.m_knownBiome (decomp\Player.cs:314)
- **Trigger:** Type `resetbiomes` in the in-game console.
- **Consequence:** ArgumentException from FieldInfo.SetValue ("Object of type 'System.Collections.Generic.HashSet`1[Heightmap+Biome]' cannot be converted to type 'System.Collections.Generic.HashSet`1[System.String]'"), the command does nothing and the exception surfaces in the console/log.
- **Evidence:** OLD: `private readonly HashSet<Heightmap.Biome> m_knownBiome = new HashSet<Heightmap.Biome>();` (decomp_old_assembly_valheim\Player.cs:199). NEW: `private readonly HashSet<string> m_knownBiome = new HashSet<string>();` (decomp\Player.cs:314), used as `m_knownBiome.Contains(biome.GetName())` in IsBiomeKnown/AddKnownBiome. Auga: `var t = typeof(Player).GetField(nameof(Player.m_knownBiome), BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic); t.SetValue(Player.m_localPlayer, new HashSet<Heightmap.Biome>());`
- **Fix:** Change the payload to `new HashSet<string>()`, and null-check `t` before SetValue.

### [inventory-2] InventoryGrid.CanDropDragOntoItem is never assigned on Auga's replacement grids — next NRE in UpdateGui after the UIDragHandler fix

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 92)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\PlayerInventory_Setup.cs:42-44 and :51-53 (m_playerGrid / m_containerGrid replaced, only m_onSelected and m_onRightClick re-attached)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** InventoryGrid.UpdateGui — scratchpad\decomp\InventoryGrid.cs:375; assignment site InventoryGui.Awake — scratchpad\decomp\InventoryGui.cs:404-407
- **Trigger:** Any frame the inventory is open and the grid holds at least one item, i.e. immediately after slots start being created.
- **Consequence:** NullReferenceException on `element.m_canBeDroppedOn = CanDropDragOntoItem(item);` — the Func<ItemDrop.ItemData,bool> is invoked with no null guard, once per item, every frame. Unlike the UIDragHandler crash this one repeats forever (the element-creation block has already been passed), so the inventory floods the log and UpdateGui aborts part-way through every item loop: durability/equipped/quality/stack overlays and tooltips stop updating from that item onward.
- **Evidence:** 1.0 InventoryGrid.cs:375 `element.m_canBeDroppedOn = CanDropDragOntoItem(item);` — no `?.Invoke`, unlike m_onSelected/m_onReleased/m_onEnter elsewhere in the class. The field `public Func<ItemDrop.ItemData, bool> CanDropDragOntoItem;` is new in 1.0 (auga_audit\diffs\InventoryGrid.diff adds it; auga_field_diff.csv lists CanDropDragOntoItem under InventoryGrid's NewIn10). Vanilla only ever sets it in InventoryGui.Awake on the grids found by `m_player.GetComponentInChildren<InventoryGrid>()` (InventoryGui.cs:378-379, 404-407); Auga's Awake postfix then overwrites m_playerGrid/m_containerGrid with `playerInventory.Find("PlayerGrid").GetComponent<InventoryGrid>()` / `containerInventory.Find("ContainerGrid")...`, so the wired vanilla grids end up inside the hidden *_VanillaDonor branch. `grep -rn "CanDropDragOntoItem" Auga/ AugaUnityLib/` returns zero hits. PortCarryOver cannot help: it only copies UnityEngine.Object references/lists (PortCarryOver.cs:104-120), never delegates, and it only iterates components on the donor ROOT (PortCarryOver.cs:62) while InventoryGrid sits on the child PlayerGrid/ContainerGrid.
- **Fix:** In InventoryGui_Awake_Patch.Postfix, after each grid reassignment, re-attach the delegate vanilla lost: `__instance.m_playerGrid.CanDropDragOntoItem += __instance.CanDropDragOntoItem;` and the same for `m_containerGrid` (both members are private in 1.0, so this needs the publicized assembly Auga already references). Safer still: capture the vanilla grid before `Replace("root/Player", ...)` and copy all of m_onSelected/m_onRightClick/m_onReleased/m_onEnter/OnSetTouchSelection/CanDropDragOntoItem/OnMoveToUpperInventoryGrid/OnMoveToLowerInventoryGrid across, instead of nulling and hand-rewiring two of them.
- **Verifier's better fix:** Fix is right. Two nits: `InventoryGrid.CanDropDragOntoItem` is public in 1.0 — only `InventoryGui.CanDropDragOntoItem` (decomp/InventoryGui.cs:488) is private, so only the InventoryGui side needs the publicized reference. And line 375 is the LAST statement of the per-item loop, so the first item is fully drawn; the abort loses items 2..n plus the whole trailing block (m_dropFocusFader, per-element m_selected, and the clear-unused-element pass at :381-400), i.e. stale icons rather than partial per-item updates.

### [harmony-1] Menu.m_skipButton changed GameObject->Button, so PortCarryOver never replaces it and Menu.SetButtonsEnabled throws - the pause menu cannot be opened

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 92)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\PauseMenu_Setup.cs:237-255 (Menu_Start_Patch.Postfix) together with C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\PortCarryOver.cs:104-111 (FillComponent only fills fields that are null); prefab C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\AugaMenu.prefab:450
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Menu.SetButtonsEnabled() (decomp\Menu.cs), called unconditionally from Menu.Show(); Menu.Show() is called from Menu.Update()
- **Trigger:** Press Escape in-game to open the pause menu (every time).
- **Consequence:** NullReferenceException in UnityEngine.Component.get_gameObject() inside Menu.SetButtonsEnabled, thrown before the menu is shown. The pause menu never appears, so Settings / Save / Logout / Quit are unreachable.
- **Evidence:** OLD Menu.cs declared `public GameObject m_skipButton;` (Menu.diff: `-	public GameObject m_skipButton;`); 1.0 declares `public Button m_skipButton;` - same name, different type, and no [FormerlySerializedAs]. AugaMenu.prefab:450 is `m_skipButton: {fileID: 4824936238869214461}` and line 3217 is `--- !u!1 &4824936238869214461 stripped` (!u!1 = GameObject), whereas the other button fields point at `!u!114` (e.g. `saveButton: {fileID: 2623661348233273495}` -> line 2919 `--- !u!114 &2623661348233273495 stripped`). PortCarryOver.FillComponent only assigns when `(Object)targetValue == null`, so a non-null wrong-typed reference is skipped - confirmed by auga_run3.log:305 / auga_run4.log, which list the filled fields as `CurrentPlayersPrefab, m_continueButton, m_inviteButton, m_settingsButton, m_logoutButton, m_quitButton, m_cloudStorageWarning, m_cloudStorageWarningNextSave, m_feedbackPrefab, m_gamepadRoot, m_gamepadMapController` - m_skipButton is absent. auga_run4.log stack: `UnityEngine.Component.get_gameObject () / Menu.SetButtonsEnabled () / Menu.Show () / Menu.Update ()`. SetButtonsEnabled's first statement is `m_skipButton.gameObject.SetActive(...)`.
- **Fix:** In PauseMenu_Setup.Menu_Start_Patch.Postfix, after PortCarryOver.InstantiateFilled, explicitly assign `newMenu.m_skipButton = donor.GetComponent<Menu>().m_skipButton;` (or the Button on Auga's own SkipIntro entry). Better: make PortCarryOver.FillComponent also overwrite a reference whose runtime type is not assignable to field.FieldType (`donorValue != null && !(targetValue is null) && !field.FieldType.IsInstanceOfType(targetValue)`), and re-serialise AugaMenu.prefab against the 1.0 Menu layout.

### [hud-build-4] KeyHints.m_radialKeyHints (new KeyHintsRadial) is dereferenced unguarded in Awake's call chain; only the donor fill keeps Auga's KeyHints copy from throwing, and the filled component is permanently inactive

- **Verdict:** partly (reviewer confidence 88) - crash already prevented by PortCarryOver
- **Auga:** Auga/Hud_Setup.cs:201 plus the KeyHints MonoBehaviour block in AugaUnity/Assets/Prefabs/HUD.prefab (m_Script fileID 1160491446, guid c1b78fa918b030faf1c1f6f6164daeb2) - no m_radialKeyHints key
- **Vanilla 1.0:** KeyHints.SetGamePadBindings (scratchpad/decomp/KeyHints.cs:109) and KeyHints.UpdateHints (decomp/KeyHints.cs:301-304); field declared at decomp/KeyHints.cs:54
- **Trigger:** Instantiation of Auga's KeyHints replacement during Hud_Awake_Postfix (Awake -> ApplySettings -> SetGamePadBindings), and thereafter every frame in UpdateHints.
- **Consequence:** Without the carry-over: NullReferenceException inside KeyHints.Awake, thrown from inside Object.Instantiate in Hud_Setup.cs:201, which would abort the rest of Hud_Awake_Postfix (ShipHud replacement, UpdateStatBars, Localize) and leave the HUD half-built. With the carry-over: no exception, but m_radialKeyHints points at the donor's `hudroot/KeyHints_VanillaDonor/RadialHints` component, whose GameObject is inactive, so `if (m_radialKeyHints.isActiveAndEnabled)` is always false and radial key hints never update.
- **Evidence:** `m_radialKeyHints.UpdateGamepadHints();` is the last statement of SetGamePadBindings with no null test (decomp/KeyHints.cs:109), and `ApplySettings()` -> `SetGamePadBindings()` is called straight from `Awake` (decomp/KeyHints.cs:58-63, 143-147). 1.0 dropped `m_radialInteract` / `m_radialBack` / `m_radialBackHint` (all present and `{fileID: 0}` in Auga's prefab) and folded them into the new `public KeyHintsRadial m_radialKeyHints;`. The donor target is `dumps/Hud_vanilla.txt:518` `hudroot/KeyHints/RadialHints  (inactive)  [UIInputHint, KeyHintsRadial]`. The fill is what saves it: PortCarryOver.InstantiateFilled holds Awake back by instantiating the prefab inactive, calling Fill, then SetActive (Auga/PortCarryOver.cs:35-52), and run4 line 253 lists `m_radialKeyHints` among the five filled fields. Note the escape is narrow: any Replace called with ReplaceFlags without DestroyOriginal, or any future change that destroys the donor, re-exposes the NRE.
- **Fix:** Add a `RadialHints` child with a `KeyHintsRadial` component (fields m_gamepadInteract/Back/Drop/DropMulti/Close/CloseTopLevel and m_kbInteract/Back/Drop/DropMulti/Close/CloseTopLevel) to Auga's KeyHints prefab and assign `m_radialKeyHints`, rather than relying on the donor fill for a field that vanilla dereferences during Awake.
- **Verifier correction:** The mechanism is right, but the without-the-carry-over consequence is wrong: Unity catches exceptions thrown from Awake during Object.Instantiate and logs them without rethrowing into the caller, so Hud_Awake_Postfix was NOT aborted. auga_run1.log proves it — the NRE stack (lines 268-278, not line 497) shows KeyHints.Awake -> ApplySettings -> SetGamePadBindings under UnityEngine.Object:Instantiate <- Auga.AugaExtensions:Replace <- Auga.Hud_Setup:Hud_Awake_Postfix, and the very next log line is the `[PortDiagnostics] Hud: 0 destroyed, 15 unassigned` postfix that only runs once Hud.Awake's patch chain completes. Effective severity with the carry-over in place is 'missing' (radial hints never update), not 'crash'.
- **Verifier's better fix:** Fix is right. Note it overlaps world-ui-3 — one RadialHints child with a KeyHintsRadial wired to BOTH m_radialHints and m_radialKeyHints resolves both findings.

### [pause-texts-3] Menu.m_settingsPrefab still points at AugaSettings.prefab, so the pause-menu Settings button instantiates Auga's 2023 Settings screen even though the port leaves Settings vanilla

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 88)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\AugaMenu.prefab:446 (`m_settingsPrefab: {fileID: 6379034644791463747, guid: 0ac5c3283b1cf6444bd7417013918aa6}` = Prefabs/AugaSettings.prefab); button wiring at AugaMenu.prefab:3792 (`m_MethodName: OnSettings`)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Menu.OnSettings() (decomp\Menu.cs:411) -> Settings.Awake()/InitializeTabs()/SetAvailableTabs() (decomp\Settings.cs:50, :88, :65)
- **Trigger:** Open the pause menu and click 'Settings'.
- **Consequence:** `SetAvailableTabs()` adds `tab.m_page.gameObject.GetComponent<ISettingsTab>()` for each of Auga's 2023 tab pages - none of which implement ISettingsTab - so SettingsTabs is a list of nulls and the next loop `settingsTab.Initialize()` throws NullReferenceException inside Settings.Awake. The screen shows a dead 2023 layout. Worse, Menu.m_settingsInstance is still assigned and m_closeMenuState becomes SettingsOpen, so Menu.Update's Escape branch is gated off (`!m_settingsInstance`), while Settings.Update's own Escape -> OnBack() -> ResetTabSettings() re-throws on the same null list: the pause menu is soft-locked.
- **Evidence:** auga_field_diff.csv row "Settings": InPrefab 90, In10 6, Dropped 87 (m_sensitivitySlider ... ConsoleAccessabilityPage), New `m_tabKeyHints, m_tabHandler, SettingsClosed`. AugaSettings.prefab line 3903 carries `m_Script: {fileID: -721420131, guid: c1b78fa918b030faf1c1f6f6164daeb2}` = assembly_valheim:Settings (fileid_map.json). decomp\Settings.cs:67-70 `foreach (TabHandler.Tab tab in m_tabHandler.m_tabs) SettingsTabs.Add(tab.m_page.gameObject.GetComponent<ISettingsTab>());` then :92-96 `foreach (ISettingsTab settingsTab in SettingsTabs) { settingsTab.Initialize(); ... }`. The README states Settings is deliberately left vanilla (Settings_Setup.cs excluded from the build) - this prefab reference defeats that, and PortCarryOver never touches it because the field is non-null.
- **Fix:** Null out `m_settingsPrefab` in AugaMenu.prefab (PortCarryOver will then fill it from the Menu donor's vanilla settings prefab), or assign it explicitly in Menu_Start_Patch.Postfix: `newMenu.m_settingsPrefab = donor.GetComponent<Menu>().m_settingsPrefab;`.

### [widgets-1] Localization.GetBoundKeyString throws KeyNotFoundException for the "JoyRStickLeft"/"JoyRStickRight" button defs, and Auga's hover-text key cache calls it for every entry of ZInput.m_buttons

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 88)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\Hud_Setup.cs:535-545 (`if (_cachedKeyNames.Count == 0) { foreach (var buttonEntry in ZInput.instance.m_buttons) { var bindingDisplay = Localization.instance.GetBoundKeyString(buttonEntry.Key); ...`)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** ZInput.GetBoundKeyString (scratchpad\decomp_assembly_utils\ZInput.cs:2142-2217), ZInput.MapKeyFromPath (ZInput.cs:2660), ZInput.s_gamepadSpriteMap (ZInput.cs:387-618), ZInput.AddGenericGamepadButtons (ZInput.cs:3086-3087); wrapper Localization.GetBoundKeyString (decomp_assembly_guiutils\Localization.cs:471)
- **Trigger:** Any hover text that contains a bracketed key token (`[E] Use`, `[LMB] Attack` ... i.e. essentially every interactable in the world) the first time Auga builds `_cachedKeyNames`, and again after every `ZInput.Save` (the `ZInput_Save_Patch` postfix at Hud_Setup.cs:573 clears the cache, so it rebuilds after any settings change).
- **Consequence:** `KeyNotFoundException: The given key 'rightStick_left' was not present in the dictionary.` thrown out of `Localization.GetBoundKeyString`, aborting Auga's hover-text construction mid-loop. `_cachedKeyNames` stays empty (`Count == 0`), so the loop re-enters and re-throws on every hover; the bracketed key glyph lines of the hover text never render.
- **Evidence:** ZInput.cs:3086 `AddButton("JoyRStickLeft", s_gamepadInputPathMap[GamepadInput.StickRLeft], altKey: false, showHints: false, rebindable: false, 0.3f, 0.1f);` and :3087 the same for `JoyRStickRight`. `s_gamepadInputPathMap[GamepadInput.StickRLeft] = "<Gamepad>/rightStick/left"`. `MapKeyFromPath` (ZInput.cs:2660): `string[] array = path.Split("/").Skip(1).ToArray(); string text = array[^1]; return string.Join("_", (text == "x" || text == "y") ? array[..^1] : array);` -> key = `"rightStick_left"`. The gamepad-source branch ends with `return "<sprite=\"" + text3 + "\" name=\"" + s_gamepadSpriteMap[key][text3] + "\">";`. `s_gamepadSpriteMap`'s complete key set is: buttonSouth, buttonEast, buttonWest, buttonNorth, leftShoulder, rightShoulder, select, start, leftStickPress, rightStickPress, leftStick_left, leftStick_right, leftStick_up, leftStick_down, leftTrigger, rightTrigger, dpad_left, dpad_right, dpad_down, dpad_up, dpad, leftStick, rightStick, rightStick_up, rightStick_down, touchpadButton - there is no `rightStick_left` or `rightStick_right` (only ZInput.cs:588 `"rightStick"` and :597 `"rightStick_up"` match a grep). `AddButton` (ZInput.cs:2926-2939) sets `inputSource = path.Contains("Gamepad") ? InputSource.Gamepad : ...` and `displayNameOverride = null`, so neither of the two early-outs in GetBoundKeyString applies. Registration is unconditional: ZInput ctor (:1517) -> Load() (:1646) -> Reset() (:1628) -> UpdateGamepadInputLayout() (:1632) -> AddGenericGamepadButtons() (:3072). The 0.217 GetBoundKeyString (decomp_old_assembly_utils\ZInput.cs:1027) had no dictionary indexing and returned `"MISSING KEY BINDING \""+name+"\""` for anything unknown, so this loop was safe before.
- **Fix:** In Hud_Setup.cs:537 skip gamepad defs and guard the call: `foreach (var buttonEntry in ZInput.instance.m_buttons) { if (buttonEntry.Value.Source == ZInput.InputSource.Gamepad) continue; string bindingDisplay; try { bindingDisplay = Localization.instance.GetBoundKeyString(buttonEntry.Key); } catch { continue; } ... }`. Auga only ever looks the cache up with keyboard/mouse bracket text (`textInBracket`), so dropping gamepad entries loses nothing.

### [harmony-2] PortInventoryElement does not add the new UIDragHandler, so InventoryGrid.UpdateGui still NREs on every Auga slot rebuild

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 88)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\PortInventoryElement.cs:15-50 (Setup adds only InventoryElement, Button, UITooltip and stand-in parts)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** InventoryGrid.UpdateGui (decomp\InventoryGrid.cs:245ff), the block that runs when m_width/m_height change
- **Trigger:** Open the inventory (or any container) so a grid is rebuilt - InventoryGui.UpdateInventory -> InventoryGrid.UpdateInventory -> UpdateGui.
- **Consequence:** NullReferenceException every frame the grid is rebuilt; the inventory grid is left half-built. This is the exception still present in auga_run4.log after the first PortInventoryElement fix.
- **Evidence:** 1.0 UpdateGui: `UIDragHandler componentInChildren2 = obj.GetComponentInChildren<UIDragHandler>(); componentInChildren2.m_onBeginDrag = (Action<UIDragHandler>)Delegate.Combine(componentInChildren2.m_onBeginDrag, new Action<UIDragHandler>(OnBeginDrag));` - unguarded. `UIDragHandler` exists only in decomp_assembly_guiutils\UIDragHandler.cs and has NO counterpart in decomp_old_assembly_guiutils, i.e. it is new in 1.0, so Auga's 2023 InventoryElement.prefab cannot reference it (its assembly_guiutils script refs are guid d920e4723ba2566674efb2f0c3eb6edb fileIDs -481414458 / 943462877 / 438561839 only). auga_run4.log: `NullReferenceException ... (wrapper dynamic-method) InventoryGrid.DMD<InventoryGrid::UpdateGui>(InventoryGrid,Player,ItemDrop/ItemData) / InventoryGrid.UpdateInventory / InventoryGui.UpdateInventory / InventoryGui.Update`, logged immediately after `[PortDiagnostics] InventoryElement added to slot prefab 'InventoryElement'`.
- **Fix:** In PortInventoryElement.Setup also do `if (prefab.GetComponentInChildren<UIDragHandler>(true) == null) prefab.AddComponent<UIDragHandler>();` (before the InventoryElement wiring), and while there confirm the prefab still has a `UIInputHandler` and a child named `binding`, which 1.0 UpdateGui also dereferences unguarded.

### [harmony-3] StoreGui.FillList postfix dereferences TradeItem.m_prefab, which 1.0 allows to be null for player-key shop entries

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 85)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\Store_Setup.cs:95-112 (FillList_Postfix)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** StoreGui.FillList (decomp\StoreGui.cs) and Trader.TradeItem (decomp\Trader.cs:8-34)
- **Trigger:** Talk to a trader whose item list contains an entry with no item prefab (1.0's `[Header("Player Key Item")]` entries, e.g. the `m_incrementKey == "invrows"` inventory-row purchase).
- **Consequence:** NullReferenceException in Auga's postfix right after the store list is built, so the store window is left without tooltips and the exception repeats on every Show/buy/sell (each calls FillList).
- **Evidence:** Auga: `var items = __instance.m_trader.GetAvailableItems(); ... var item = items[index]; itemElement.GetComponent<ItemTooltip>().Item = item.m_prefab.m_itemData;` - no null test. 1.0 FillList guards every use: `if ((bool)tradeItem.m_icon) ... else if (tradeItem.m_prefab != null)`, `Localization.instance.Localize(tradeItem.m_prefab ? tradeItem.m_prefab.m_itemData.m_shared.m_name : tradeItem.m_name)`, `else if ((bool)tradeItem.m_prefab) component3.Set(...)`. Trader.TradeItem gained `public Sprite m_icon; public string m_name; public string m_tooltip; public string m_buyKey; public string m_incrementKey; public int m_incrementAmount;` under `[Header("Player Key Item")]`, and StoreGui.BuySelectedItem in 1.0 is explicitly `if (m_selectedItem.m_prefab != null) {...}` / `if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_selectedItem.m_buyKey)) {...}`.
- **Fix:** Guard the postfix the way vanilla does: `var tooltip = itemElement.GetComponent<ItemTooltip>(); if (tooltip != null) tooltip.Item = item.m_prefab != null ? item.m_prefab.m_itemData : null;` and have Auga's ComplexTooltip fall back to item.m_name / item.m_tooltip / item.m_icon when Item is null.
- **Verifier's better fix:** Fix is right. The one thing I cannot verify is whether any shipped trader actually ships a null-prefab entry — that is in the game's asset data, not in the decompiles — but 1.0 adding the field set and guarding all four use sites makes it the intended data shape.

### [minimap-3] MinimapShowPinNameInputTranspiler.AddSubmitAction NREs on touch input: 1.0 no longer activates the NameField before the injection point and GetComponentInChildren skips inactive objects

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 82)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\Minimap_Setup.cs:20 (`instance.m_nameInput.GetComponentInChildren<GuiInputFieldSubmit>().m_onSubmit = instance.OnPinTextEntered;`), injected at Minimap_Setup.cs:52-70 immediately before `m_wasFocused = true`
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Minimap.ShowPinNameInput (scratchpad\decomp\Minimap.cs:809-840, specifically the new guard at :813-816)
- **Trigger:** On a touchscreen (ZInput.IsTouchActive() true), double-tap the large map to add a pin.
- **Consequence:** NullReferenceException thrown from inside Minimap.ShowPinNameInput, so `m_wasFocused = true` never runs and the whole pin-naming flow aborts after AddPin — InTextInput() stays false, the virtual keyboard coroutine has already been started, and the map is left in an inconsistent input state.
- **Evidence:** 1.0 wrapped the activation in a guard: `if (!ZInput.IsTouchActive()) { m_nameInput.gameObject.SetActive(value: true); }` (decomp\Minimap.cs:813-816). 0.217.30 activated it unconditionally — `((Component)m_nameInput).gameObject.SetActive(true);` at decomp_old_assembly_valheim\Minimap.cs:615, with no touch branch at all. The transpiler injects the call after that block (it matches on the final `stfld Minimap::m_wasFocused`), and `Component.GetComponentInChildren<T>()` defaults to includeInactive=false, so on an inactive NameField it returns null even for the component on the same GameObject. Auga's NameField ships inactive: HUD.prefab override `propertyPath: m_IsActive value: 0` on TextField root fileID 4754684309381547899.
- **Fix:** Use the inactive-inclusive overload and null-guard: `var submit = instance.m_nameInput.GetComponentInChildren<GuiInputFieldSubmit>(true); if (submit != null) submit.m_onSubmit = instance.OnPinTextEntered;` (GuiInputFieldSubmit is on the NameField root, so `GetComponent<GuiInputFieldSubmit>()` also works and is cheaper).
- **Verifier's better fix:** Fix is right; `GetComponent<GuiInputFieldSubmit>()` is indeed sufficient since the component is on the NameField root. Worth pairing with auga-lib-5 though: GuiInputFieldSubmit.Update polls legacy UnityEngine.Input and calls ActivateInputField() every frame, so even once the null is fixed the component is the wrong mechanism — subscribing to GuiInputField.OnInputSubmit removes both problems.

### [world-ui-1] Store_Setup.FillList postfix dereferences TradeItem.m_prefab, which 1.0 allows to be null for "Player Key Item" entries

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 78)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\Store_Setup.cs:104-110 (FillList_Postfix); line 109: itemElement.GetComponent<ItemTooltip>().Item = item.m_prefab.m_itemData;
- **Vanilla 1.0:** StoreGui.FillList (decomp\StoreGui.cs:235-301) and Trader.TradeItem (decomp\Trader.cs)
- **Trigger:** Player interacts with a trader (Haldor/Hildir) whose item list contains a trade item with no m_prefab - the 1.0 "Player Key Item" form (m_buyKey / m_incrementKey, e.g. the inventory-row upgrade). Re-thrown after every buy and every sell, because both call FillList().
- **Consequence:** NullReferenceException thrown out of the FillList postfix, propagating out of StoreGui.Show and out of Trader.Interact. The panel is already active and the vanilla list is already built, so the shop appears, but the trader's greeting Say()/m_randomStartTradeFX never run, every item after the offending one loses its Auga ComplexTooltip, and each buy/sell logs another NRE.
- **Evidence:** 1.0 added to Trader.TradeItem: `[Header("Player Key Item")] public Sprite m_icon; public string m_name; public string m_tooltip; public string m_buyKey; public string m_incrementKey; public int m_incrementAmount = 1;`. 1.0's own FillList guards m_prefab four times: `if ((bool)tradeItem.m_icon) ... else if (tradeItem.m_prefab != null)`; `Localization.instance.Localize(tradeItem.m_prefab ? tradeItem.m_prefab.m_itemData.m_shared.m_name : tradeItem.m_name)`; `else if ((bool)tradeItem.m_prefab)` for the tooltip. BuySelectedItem is now `if (m_selectedItem.m_prefab != null) { ... }` plus a key-only branch containing `if (m_selectedItem.m_incrementKey == "invrows") Player.m_localPlayer.SetInventorySize(num);`. UpdateBuyButton: `bool flag2 = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_selectedItem.m_incrementKey) && m_selectedItem.m_prefab == null;`. Trader.DiscoverItems now wraps `if ((bool)availableItem.m_prefab) player.AddKnownItem(...)`. decomp\Player.cs:109 `public const string InventoryRowsKey = "invrows";` and decomp\Player.cs:5039 SetInventorySize confirm the feature is live. Auga's line has no guard.
- **Fix:** In Store_Setup.FillList_Postfix bound the loop by Math.Min(items.Count, __instance.m_itemList.Count) and null-check both the component and the prefab: `var t = itemElement.GetComponent<ItemTooltip>(); if (t != null) t.Item = item.m_prefab != null ? item.m_prefab.m_itemData : null;`. Leaving Item null is the correct fallback - UITooltip_UpdateTextElements_Patch then falls through to customTooltip.SetDefault(__instance), which shows the topic/text vanilla FillList already set from tradeItem.m_name / tradeItem.m_tooltip.

## Severity: broken

### [crafting-2] AugaCraftingPanel.OnUpdateRecipe divides by m_craftDuration only - the progress bar is wrong for multi-craft, upgrader stations and skilled crafters

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 95)
- **Auga:** AugaUnityLib/AugaCraftingPanel.cs:177-188, line 182: `var percent = instance.m_craftTimer / instance.m_craftDuration;`
- **Vanilla 1.0:** InventoryGui.UpdateRecipe (decomp/InventoryGui.cs:1625-1639)
- **Trigger:** Hold AltPlace (or left stick) while pressing Craft to multi-craft x5; or craft at any station with m_craftingSkill set once the player has crafting skill; or use a station with m_upgrader = true.
- **Consequence:** The visible Auga bar (`CraftProgressBar.fillAmount`) fills against the wrong denominator. A multi-craft runs for `m_multiCraftDuration` (6s default) but Auga's bar hits 100% after `m_craftDuration` (2s) and then sits full for four more seconds; an upgrader craft runs for `m_upgraderDuration + quality * m_upgraderDurationPerLevel` (8s+) with the same result. Conversely a high-skill craft can finish in 40% of m_craftDuration, so the bar jumps from partial straight to done. The vanilla GuiBar that would be correct is pointed at `CraftingPanel.DummyCraftProgressBar` (Auga/PlayerInventory_Setup.cs:115), a hidden dummy, so the player only ever sees Auga's wrong bar.
- **Evidence:** 1.0: `float num6 = (flag ? (m_upgraderDuration + (float)num * m_upgraderDurationPerLevel) : (m_multiCrafting ? m_multiCraftDuration : m_craftDuration)); if (currentCraftingStation != null && currentCraftingStation.m_craftingSkill != Skills.SkillType.None) { num6 *= 1f - Player.m_localPlayer.GetSkillFactor(currentCraftingStation.m_craftingSkill) * m_craftDurationSkillMaxDecrease; } ... m_craftProgressBar.SetMaxValue(num6); ... if (m_craftTimer >= num6)`. 0.217 had only `m_craftProgressBar.SetMaxValue(m_craftDuration)` (decomp_old_assembly_valheim/InventoryGui.cs:1276). auga_field_diff.csv lists m_multiCraftDuration, m_upgraderDuration, m_upgraderDurationPerLevel and m_craftDurationSkillMaxDecrease as New for InventoryGui; no Auga file mentions any of them.
- **Fix:** Either replicate 1.0's duration formula in OnUpdateRecipe, or simpler: drive the Auga bar from the vanilla GuiBar that vanilla just filled in (the postfix runs after UpdateRecipe), e.g. `percent = instance.m_craftProgressBar.m_value / instance.m_craftProgressBar.m_maxValue`.

### [hud-build-1] Auga skins Hud.m_pieceSelectionWindow, which 1.0 deactivates in Awake and never shows again - the real 1.0 build menu is the untouched hudroot/BuildUIV2 (BuildUi)

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 95)
- **Auga:** Auga/Hud_Setup.cs:143-199 (whole build-hud block: Darken/Bkg2 replace, Categories restyle, m_pieceIconPrefab swap, m_pieceListRoot re-point)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Hud.Awake / Hud.TogglePieceSelection / Hud.UpdateBuild (scratchpad/decomp/Hud.cs:382, 1412-1423, 1448-1509)
- **Trigger:** Player equips a hammer/hoe/cultivator and presses the build-menu key.
- **Consequence:** No exception. Auga's build menu never appears; the player sees the stock Valheim 1.0 BuildUIV2 (tabs 'By Usage / By Material / Recent / Favorites', search bar, tag list, scrolling grid) with vanilla art next to Auga's restyled HUD. Everything Auga built for the build menu - restyled background, darken layer, gold category text, BuildHudElement piece icons, the paged tab strip - is invisible.
- **Evidence:** In 1.0 `m_pieceSelectionWindow` is referenced exactly twice in the whole decompile: the declaration `public GameObject m_pieceSelectionWindow;` (decomp/Hud.cs:66) and `m_pieceSelectionWindow.SetActive(value: false);` in Awake (decomp/Hud.cs:382). The 0.217.30 source had eight references including `m_pieceSelectionWindow.SetActive(true)` in `TogglePieceSelection` (decomp_old_assembly_valheim/Hud.cs:1191) and `return m_instance.m_pieceSelectionWindow.activeSelf` in `IsPieceSelectionVisible` (line 1217). 1.0 replaced both with the new field `public BuildUi m_buildUi;` (decomp/Hud.cs:59): `TogglePieceSelection` now calls `m_buildUi.OpenBuildMenu()` / `m_buildUi.Close()`, `IsPieceSelectionVisible` returns `m_instance.m_buildUi.gameObject.activeSelf`, and `UpdateBuild` gates on `bool activeSelf = m_buildUi.gameObject.activeSelf;`. The live hierarchy confirms the two objects exist side by side: `dumps/Hud_vanilla.txt:95` `hudroot/BuildHud  (inactive)  []` with the legacy `hudroot/BuildHud/bar/SelectionWindow` (line 108) that Auga skins, and `dumps/Hud_vanilla.txt:252` `hudroot/BuildUIV2  [BuildUi, TabHandler]`, which no line of Auga source mentions (grep for 'BuildUi' in Auga/ and AugaUnityLib/ returns only Auga's own BuildHud/BuildHudElement/BuildMenuPagination names). That `m_pieceSelectionWindow` really is the legacy object and not BuildUIV2's is proven by the run log: `Hud_Setup.cs:162` does `m_pieceSelectionWindow.transform.Find("Categories")` and then dereferences `categories.childCount`; BuildUIV2's window has `TabContainer`, not `Categories`, so if it were that object the postfix would have thrown before line 201 - yet run4 prints the line-201 carry-over message `[PortDiagnostics] KeyHints on 'KeyHints(Clone)': 5 empty references filled...`.
- **Fix:** Re-target the build skin at the new menu: style `hudroot/BuildUIV2/bar/SelectionWindow` (Background, TabContainer/Tabs/{By Usage,By Material,Recent,Favorites}, Main View/Root/Piece List Container, Main View/Root/TagList) and replace `BuildUi.m_pieceButtonPrefab` / `m_tagButtonPrefab` (private [SerializeField] on BuildUi) with Auga versions, instead of `Hud.m_pieceIconPrefab` / `m_pieceListRoot`. Until then, delete the dead block at Hud_Setup.cs:143-199 so the mod stops instantiating 90 invisible BuildHudElements per build session and stops mutating the vanilla SelectionWindow.

### [hud-bars-1] 1.0's new adrenaline bar runs on the untouched vanilla hudroot/adrenalinepanel; Auga has no adrenaline UI and never moves or restyles it

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 92)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\Hud_Setup.cs:90-131 (Hud_Awake_Postfix stat-bar teardown) and AugaUnity/Assets/Prefabs/HUD.prefab (hudroot has HealthBar, StaminaBar, EitrBar, FoodPanel0-2 — no adrenaline panel)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Hud.UpdateAdrenaline (decomp/Hud.cs:1122), called unconditionally from Hud.Update (decomp/Hud.cs:534); fields Hud.m_adrenalineBarRoot/m_adrenalineAnimator/m_adrenalineBarFast/m_adrenalineBarSlow/m_adrenalineText (decomp/Hud.cs:178-186); Hud.SetAdrenalineBarSize; Hud.AdrenalineBarFlash called from Player.AddAdrenaline (decomp/Player.cs:4594)
- **Trigger:** Any combat. Player.m_maxAdrenaline defaults to 100f (decomp/Player.cs:229) so GetMaxAdrenaline() > 0 always; as soon as adrenaline rises above 0 UpdateAdrenaline stops early-returning and turns the panel on.
- **Consequence:** No exception. A vanilla-art adrenaline bar fades in at the vanilla bottom-of-screen slot that Auga has emptied (Auga destroyed staminapanel/eitrpanel and moved its own bars to the lower-left), colliding with Auga's StaggerPanel (LowerCenter, y=151) and ActionProgress (LowerCenter, y=226). It carries no MovableHudElement, so the player cannot reposition or hide it from Auga's config, and Auga's own stat-bar stack has no adrenaline readout at all.
- **Evidence:** Auga's Hud_Awake_Postfix destroys `__instance.m_staminaBar2Root.gameObject` and `__instance.m_eitrBarRoot.gameObject` and every child of `m_healthPanel`, then CopyOvers `hudroot/HealthBar`, `hudroot/StaminaBar`, `hudroot/EitrBar`, `hudroot/FoodPanel0..2` — adrenaline is never mentioned. `grep -rin adrenaline Auga/ AugaUnityLib/` returns zero hits. The 1.0 dump (auga_audit/dumps/Hud_vanilla.txt:15-24) shows `hudroot/adrenalinepanel  [Animator]` (ACTIVE, no "(inactive)" marker) with `hudroot/adrenalinepanel/Stamina/adrenaline_slow [GuiBar]`, `adrenaline_fast [GuiBar]`, `adrenalineText [TextMeshProUGUI]` as a sibling of staminapanel/eitrpanel/healthpanel. `[PortDiagnostics] Hud: 0 destroyed, 15 unassigned` (auga_run4.log:254-255) lists only m_healthBarRoot, m_healthBarFast, m_healthBarSlow, m_healthText, m_foodBarRoot, m_foodBaseBar, m_foodText, m_staminaBar2Root, m_staminaBar2Fast, m_staminaBar2Slow, m_staminaText, m_eitrBarRoot, m_eitrBarFast, m_eitrBarSlow, m_eitrText — no m_adrenaline* field, proving they are still wired to the live vanilla panel. auga_field_diff.csv lists m_adrenalineBarRoot/m_adrenalineAnimator/m_adrenalineBarFast/m_adrenalineBarSlow/m_adrenalineText among the 21 fields "NewIn10" that Auga's own serialized Hud copy lacks.
