NEW: `bool flag = m_text != "" || m_topic != ""; if ((bool)m_current && !flag) { HideTooltip(); } if (flag) { if (m_tooltip == null) { m_tooltip = Object.Instantiate(m_tooltipPrefab, base.transform.GetComponentInParent<Canvas>().transform); } UpdateTextElements(); ... }` - the unconditional HideTooltip is gone and the Instantiate is now guarded by `m_tooltip == null`, and `m_tooltip` is `private static`. The gamepad path that reaches it without an exit: `if (m_gamepadFocusObject.activeSelf && m_current != this) { OnHoverStart(m_gamepadFocusObject); }`. Prefab structure confirmed with the audit's parse_prefab on InventoryTooltip.prefab: `InventoryTooltip/Tooltip/Titles/Topic`, `InventoryTooltip/Tooltip/TextBoxContainer/TextBox/Text`, and ComplexTooltip.cs:141 `TwoColumnTextBoxPrefab.gameObject.SetActive(false);`.
- **Fix:** Add a Harmony prefix on `UITooltip.OnHoverStart` that force-hides a mismatched instance before vanilla runs, e.g. tag each created tooltip with a marker component holding the source prefab and, if `UITooltip.m_tooltip != null && marker.source != __instance.m_tooltipPrefab`, call `UITooltip.HideTooltip()` first. Cheap equivalent: `if (UITooltip.m_tooltip != null && (UITooltip.m_tooltip.GetComponent<ComplexTooltip>() != null) != (__instance.m_tooltipPrefab == Auga.Assets.InventoryTooltip)) UITooltip.HideTooltip();`. Separately make UITooltip_Patch.cs:45 `return false;` after `SetDefault` so vanilla's UpdateTextElements never writes into the ComplexTooltip's text-box templates.

### [harmony-13] SkillsDialog_Patch stacks three [HarmonyPatch(nameof(...))] attributes on one prefix, so only the last target is actually patched

- **Verdict:** partly (reviewer confidence 70)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\SkillsDialog_Patch.cs:11-18 (Update_Prefix carries [HarmonyPatch(nameof(SkillsDialog.Update))], [HarmonyPatch(nameof(SkillsDialog.OnClose))] and [HarmonyPatch(nameof(SkillsDialog.SkillClicked))])
- **Vanilla 1.0:** SkillsDialog.Update (decomp\SkillsDialog.cs:70), SkillsDialog.OnClose (:155), SkillsDialog.SkillClicked (:160)
- **Trigger:** Open Auga's Skills tab and press the vanilla close binding, or hold a gamepad stick while it is open.
- **Consequence:** Harmony merges multiple HarmonyPatch attributes on one method into a single target spec (last non-null field wins), so only SkillsDialog.SkillClicked is prefixed. Vanilla SkillsDialog.OnClose still runs and calls `base.gameObject.SetActive(false)` on the object Auga re-parented as its Skills tab content, and vanilla Update still runs (harmless today only because Auga's transpiled Setup leaves m_elements empty, so both `if (... m_elements.Count > 0)` branches are skipped).
- **Evidence:** HarmonyLib builds the patch info via HarmonyMethod.Merge over all HarmonyAttribute instances on the member, overwriting `methodName` each time, so three attributes yield one target. Auga's Setup transpiler (SkillsDialog_Patch.cs:24-50) replaces the body with `SetupOverride(instance, player)` which is empty, so m_elements never fills. 1.0's SkillsDialog.Update additionally calls `GamepadRumble.instance.PlayGlobalSelectVibration()` and `gameObject.GetComponentInChildren<UITooltip>().OnHoverStart(gameObject)` inside that guarded block.
- **Fix:** Split into three separate prefix methods (or use a `[HarmonyPatch]` class with `TargetMethods()` yielding the three MethodInfos). This predates 1.0 but is worth fixing while the file is being ported.
- **Verifier correction:** The patch defect is real — three [HarmonyPatch(nameof(...))] attributes on one method merge into a single target spec (HarmonyMethod.Merge overwrites methodName per attribute, last non-null wins), so only one of Update/OnClose/SkillClicked is prefixed. The stated consequence is not supported, though. Nothing in Auga invokes SkillsDialog.OnClose: `grep -rn 'm_MethodName: OnClose' AugaUnity/Assets/Prefabs/*.prefab` matches only AugaMenu.prefab:359, and the vanilla close button lived on root/Skills, which PlayerInventory_Setup.cs:68 replaces. So the 'press the vanilla close binding' trigger has no path. Vanilla Update is inert for the reason the finding itself gives (m_elements stays empty). Net effect today is latent, not player-visible — severity closer to cosmetic than 'broken'.
- **Verifier's better fix:** Fix as proposed (three separate prefixes, or a [HarmonyPatch] class with TargetMethods() yielding the three MethodInfos). Worth pairing with a check of which target actually won in the current build before assuming SkillClicked — attribute enumeration order is metadata order, not a documented guarantee.

### [crafting-6] AugaCraftingPanel.SetRecipe clamps the preview quality to m_maxQuality, so the upgrade preview shows the current level at an upgrader station

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 68)
- **Auga:** AugaUnityLib/AugaCraftingPanel.cs:161-169, line 163: `var quality = Mathf.Min(item.m_quality + 1, item.m_shared.m_maxQuality); ItemInfo.SetItem(item, quality, variant);`
- **Vanilla 1.0:** InventoryGui.UpdateRecipe (decomp/InventoryGui.cs:1504-1505, 1517, 1544) and InventoryGui.DoCrafting (decomp/InventoryGui.cs:1864)
- **Trigger:** Select a max-quality item on the Upgrade tab at a station with m_upgrader = true.
- **Consequence:** Vanilla evaluates the whole panel at `num = itemData.m_quality + 1` with no clamp - at an upgrader that legitimately exceeds m_maxQuality, and DoCrafting then re-creates the item at exactly that quality. Auga's ComplexTooltip is clamped to the current max, so the stat block shown is the item the player already has, not the one they are paying for, and it no longer matches the requirement rows beneath it (which vanilla drew at the unclamped quality via `SetupRequirementList(num, ...)`).
- **Evidence:** Auga: `Mathf.Min(item.m_quality + 1, item.m_shared.m_maxQuality)`. 1.0: `num = ((itemData == null) ? 1 : (itemData.m_quality + 1));`, `bool flag2 = num <= m_selectedRecipe.Recipe.m_item.m_itemData.m_shared.m_maxQuality || (currentCraftingStation != null && currentCraftingStation.m_upgrader);`, `itemData = player.GetInventory().AddItem(m_craftRecipe.m_item.gameObject.name, num3, num, variant, playerID, playerName, position, cheated);`. The `|| currentCraftingStation.m_upgrader` clause is new in 1.0 (0.217: `bool flag = num <= m_selectedRecipe.Key.m_item.m_itemData.m_shared.m_maxQuality;`).
- **Fix:** Only clamp when the station is not an upgrader: `var station = Player.m_localPlayer?.GetCurrentCraftingStation(); var quality = (station != null && station.m_upgrader) ? item.m_quality + 1 : Mathf.Min(item.m_quality + 1, item.m_shared.m_maxQuality);`

### [text-chat-7] ChatWindowController still drives chat focus with legacy UnityEngine.Input while 1.0 moved every Chat/Terminal input read to ZInput

- **Verdict:** partly (reviewer confidence 68)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\AugaUnityLib\ChatWindowController.cs:22-24 (`if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Mouse0) || Input.GetKey(KeyCode.Mouse1) || Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape) || ZInput.GetButtonDown("JoyButtonB") || ...)`) and lines 32-42 (the re-focus block)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Chat.Update (scratchpad\decomp\Chat.cs, `if (ZInput.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Mouse0) || ZInput.GetKey(KeyCode.Mouse1) || ZInput.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape) || ...)`) and the opening gate `... && !Hud.IsPieceSelectionVisible() && DemoMode.Disabled`
- **Trigger:** Any frame the Auga chat input object is active; the piece-selection case triggers by opening the build wheel while chat is open.
- **Consequence:** Chat close/blur is decided from the legacy input path instead of ZInput, so it bypasses ZInput's remapping, its input-source arbitration and its blocking; and because the controller unconditionally calls `m_input.ActivateInputField()` every frame the field is active, it also defeats 1.0's new `m_doubleOpenForVirtualKeyboard`/`m_input.Select()` branch. It also lacks 1.0's new `!Hud.IsPieceSelectionVisible() && DemoMode.Disabled` gate.
- **Evidence:** 0.217.30 Chat.Update used `Input.GetKeyDown((KeyCode)323) || Input.GetKey((KeyCode)324) || Input.GetKeyDown((KeyCode)27)`; 1.0 rewrote exactly those three reads to `ZInput.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Mouse0) || ZInput.GetKey(KeyCode.Mouse1) || ZInput.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape)`. A grep over the whole 1.0 decomp for legacy `Input.GetKey`/`Input.mousePosition`/`Input.GetMouseButton` (excluding ZInput) returns zero hits, while the 0.217.30 decomp still had them in GameCamera, KeyHints, ManageSavesMenu, MusicMan, PlayerCustomizaton - 1.0 is on the new Input System throughout. 1.0 Chat.Update gate: `if (Player.m_localPlayer != null && !Console.IsVisible() && !TextInput.IsVisible() && !Minimap.InTextInput() && !Menu.IsVisible() && !InventoryGui.IsVisible() && !Hud.IsPieceSelectionVisible() && DemoMode.Disabled)`.
- **Fix:** Replace the three `Input.*` reads in ChatWindowController.Update with `ZInput.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Mouse0)` / `ZInput.GetKey(KeyCode.Mouse1)` / `ZInput.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape)`, add `&& !Hud.IsPieceSelectionVisible()` to the re-focus gate, and only call ActivateInputField when `!_chatHandler.m_input.isFocused`. The same legacy-Input pattern is in AugaUnityLib\GuiInputFieldSubmit.cs:17 and InputFieldSubmitEvents.cs:22.
- **Verifier correction:** The legacy-Input code is there, but it is NOT load-bearing: Auga does not patch Chat.Update. Auga/Chat_Setup.cs patches only Chat.Awake (:10) and Chat.SetNpcText (:37), and no other file patches Chat/Terminal Update (only Terminal.InitTerminal at Auga.cs:667). So vanilla Chat.Update still runs and still closes/blurs the chat through ZInput at decomp/Chat.cs:217. ChatWindowController's block at :23-26 is a redundant duplicate of that, so its dead legacy reads cost nothing for close-on-click/Escape — severity should drop from 'broken' to cosmetic. Also, the m_doubleOpenForVirtualKeyboard branch is inside `if (m_doubleOpenForVirtualKeyboard && Application.isConsolePlatform)` (decomp/Chat.cs:202), so it never runs on PC and cannot be 'defeated' there. What does survive: ChatWindowController's re-focus block (:32-42) lacks 1.0's new `!Hud.IsPieceSelectionVisible() && DemoMode.Disabled` gate and calls ActivateInputField unconditionally, so opening the build wheel with chat open keeps re-focusing the field.
- **Verifier's better fix:** Keep the ZInput replacement for hygiene, but the real fix is to delete the duplicated close block in ChatWindowController.Update (vanilla already does it correctly) and add the missing `!Hud.IsPieceSelectionVisible() && DemoMode.Disabled` conditions plus an `if (!_chatHandler.m_input.isFocused)` guard around ActivateInputField.

### [pause-texts-13] The vanilla Menu donor keeps its SavingFinished/WorldSaveFinished subscription, so Menu.SaveFinished now runs on two Menu instances

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 65)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\PauseMenu_Setup.cs:237-255 (Menu_Start_Patch.Postfix runs AFTER the vanilla Menu.Start has subscribed, then keeps that object alive via PortCarryOver.MakeDonor instead of destroying it)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Menu.Start() (decomp\Menu.cs:126-133), Menu.OnDestroy() (decomp\Menu.cs:199-204), Menu.SaveFinished() (decomp\Menu.cs:206-214)
- **Trigger:** Any world/profile save while cloud (or console) storage is low, i.e. whenever `SaveFinished` finds `!CanSaveToCloudStorage()`.
- **Consequence:** Both the donor Menu and the Auga Menu have `PlayerProfile.SavingFinished`/`ZNet.WorldSaveFinished` handlers, because the donor is only SetActive(false) and OnDestroy never runs. SaveFinished therefore fires twice, and on the low-storage path each one calls `ShowCloudStorageLowNextSaveWarning()` -> `Logout()` -> `Game.instance.Logout(...)` - a double logout request. Before the port Auga destroyed the vanilla Menu, so OnDestroy unsubscribed it.
- **Evidence:** decomp\Menu.cs:126-133 subscribes in Start; PauseMenu_Setup.cs:241-252 is a Postfix on Start, so the donor is already subscribed when MakeDonor runs. PortCarryOver.cs:23-29 only does `original.SetActive(false)` / rename / SetAsLastSibling - it never destroys, so Menu.OnDestroy (decomp\Menu.cs:199-204, the only unsubscribe) never executes. decomp\Menu.cs:611-617 ShowCloudStorageLowNextSaveWarning calls `Logout();` unconditionally.
- **Fix:** In Menu_Start_Patch.Postfix, explicitly detach the donor's handlers before hiding it (`PlayerProfile.SavingFinished -= donorMenu.SaveFinished; ZNet.WorldSaveFinished -= donorMenu.SaveFinished;`) or disable the donor's Menu component after MakeDonor so only the Auga instance is live.

### [widgets-10] PortCarryOver fills anchor/parent-typed UITooltip references from the hidden donor, which would reparent the live tooltip under an inactive object

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 64)
- **Auga:** Auga\PortCarryOver.cs:104-110 (`if ((Object)targetValue == null && (Object)donorValue != null) { field.SetValue(target, donorValue); }`) reached via Auga\SetupHelper.cs:25 / Auga\Extensions.cs:99 `PortCarryOver.InstantiateFilled(...)`; donor made inactive at PortCarryOver.cs:25 `original.SetActive(false);`
- **Vanilla 1.0:** UITooltip.AnchorTooltip (`if (m_anchor != null) { m_tooltip.transform.SetParent(m_anchor); m_tooltip.transform.localPosition = m_fixedPosition; return; }`) and UITooltip.Set (scratchpad\decomp_assembly_guiutils\UITooltip.cs); callers that pass an `m_tooltipAnchor`: SkillsDialog.Setup (decomp\SkillsDialog.cs:129), InventoryGrid.UpdateGui (decomp\InventoryGrid.cs:411), StoreGui (decomp\StoreGui.cs:278/282), FejdStartup (decomp\FejdStartup.cs:1410)
- **Trigger:** Gamepad or touch input on any screen whose vanilla component had an `m_tooltipAnchor`-style RectTransform that Auga's copy leaves null. `AnchorTooltip()` is only called from the `IsExclusiveGamepadActive() || IsTouchActive()` branch of LateUpdate, so mouse users are unaffected.
- **Consequence:** The tooltip GameObject is reparented under the inactive `*_VanillaDonor` branch and disappears - no exception, just a tooltip that never shows on a controller. Today this is latent rather than live: the one instance the logs prove (SkillsDialog.m_tooltipAnchor) is neutralised because Auga transpiles `SkillsDialog.Setup` to a no-op and prefixes `Update`/`OnClose`/`SkillClicked` to `return false`, so the anchor is never passed to `UITooltip.Set`. Any future fill of a Transform/RectTransform field that vanilla uses as a *parent* has the same failure mode.
- **Evidence:** auga_audit\logs\auga_run4.log:237 `[PortDiagnostics] SkillsDialog on 'TabContent_Skills(Clone)': 1 empty references filled from the hidden vanilla object: m_tooltipAnchor`. decomp\SkillsDialog.cs:129 `obj.GetComponentInChildren<UITooltip>().Set("", skill.m_info.m_description, m_tooltipAnchor, new Vector2(0f, Math.Min(255f, rectTransform.localPosition.y + 10f)));`. PortCarryOver.MakeDonor: `original.SetActive(false); original.name += DonorSuffix; original.transform.SetAsLastSibling();`. Neutralised by Auga\SkillsDialog_Patch.cs:11-18 and :24-50 (the `Setup` transpiler emits only `ldarg.0; ldarg.1; call SetupOverride; ret`, and `SetupOverride` is an empty body).
- **Fix:** In `PortCarryOver.FillComponent`, skip (and log instead of assigning) fields whose donor value is a Transform/RectTransform that lives under a `*_VanillaDonor` subtree - `donorTransform.GetComponentsInParent<Transform>(true).Any(t => t.name.EndsWith(PortCarryOver.DonorSuffix))` - so an anchor is left null rather than pointed at something hidden; UITooltip then falls back to its `m_fixedPosition`/world-corner path. Where the field is genuinely needed, create a visible stand-in under the Auga replacement (as PortInventoryElement already does for `dropfocus`) and assign that.

### [widgets-5] AugaUnityLib still polls legacy UnityEngine.Input, which Valheim 1.0 no longer enables - Escape/Enter/mouse dismissal in the Auga chat, compendium and input fields is dead

- **Verdict:** partly (reviewer confidence 62)
- **Auga:** AugaUnityLib\ChatWindowController.cs:23-26 (`Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Mouse0) || Input.GetKey(KeyCode.Mouse1) || Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape) || ...`), AugaUnityLib\AugaCompendiumController.cs:153 (`Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape) || ZInput.GetButtonDown("JoyMenu")`), AugaUnityLib\GuiInputFieldSubmit.cs:17, AugaUnityLib\InputFieldSubmitEvents.cs:23 (`!Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Return) && !Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.KeypadEnter)`)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** ZInput rewritten onto UnityEngine.InputSystem (scratchpad\decomp_assembly_utils\ZInput.cs:2093-2135 `GetKey/GetKeyDown/GetKeyUp` -> `TryGetKeyStateLowLevel` -> `Keyboard.current[...]`, `Mouse.current`, `Gamepad.current`); 1.0 removed every legacy `Input.` call from assembly_valheim (e.g. old Chat.cs:209 `Input.GetKeyDown((KeyCode)323) || ... || Input.GetKeyDown((KeyCode)27)` is gone)
- **Trigger:** Every frame for `ChatWindowController` (it sits on `AugaChat/AugaChat`, the same GameObject as the vanilla `Chat` component, which is always active); on Escape while the Auga compendium is open; on Enter in the Auga chat/text input fields.
- **Consequence:** Either the calls throw `InvalidOperationException: You are trying to read Input using the UnityEngine.Input class, but you have switched active Input handling to Input System package in Player Settings` every frame, or (if legacy input is left enabled but unfed) they always return false. In the latter, documented case: clicking/right-holding/pressing Escape no longer unfocuses and closes the Auga chat input, Escape no longer closes the compendium (only the gamepad `JoyMenu` path works), and Enter/NumpadEnter no longer submits an Auga input field (only `JoyButtonA` does).
- **Evidence:** Grep over decomp\*.cs, decomp_assembly_guiutils\*.cs and decomp_assembly_utils\*.cs finds exactly two legacy `Input.` call sites left in 1.0, both in the unused `InputDefinition.ReadAsAnalogue/ReadAsDigital` (decomp_assembly_utils\InputDefinition.cs:154,173); the 0.217 assembly had them all over Chat, GameCamera, KeyHints, ManageSavesMenu, MusicMan. The project memory note valheim-1-0-modding-setup records: "Valheim 1.0 uses Unity's new Input System only (Unity.InputSystem.dll, no legacy Input.GetKey calls anywhere in game code). BepInEx KeyboardShortcut/UnityInput hotkeys silently never fire." Prefab placement confirmed by parse_prefab on AugaChat.prefab: `AugaChat/AugaChat  [MBc1b78f (assembly_valheim Chat), MB045bc8 (Unity.Auga ChatWindowController)]`. Counter-evidence to the throwing variant: no InvalidOperationException appears in auga_audit\logs\auga_run1..4.log, so on this build the calls most likely return false rather than throw.
- **Fix:** Replace all four sites with ZInput / Input System equivalents: `ZInput.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape, logWarning: false)` / `ZInput.GetMouseButtonDown(0)` / `ZInput.GetMouseButton(1)` (ZInput.cs:2018-2028, 2093-2103) or `UnityEngine.InputSystem.Keyboard.current[Key.Escape].wasPressedThisFrame`. For submit, prefer `Keyboard.current.enterKey.wasPressedThisFrame || Keyboard.current.numpadEnterKey.wasPressedThisFrame`.
- **Verifier correction:** All four call sites and the 1.0 input rewrite are real, but two claims are off. (1) The chat consequence is wrong for the same reason as text-chat-7: Auga patches only Chat.Awake and Chat.SetNpcText, so vanilla Chat.Update still unfocuses/closes the chat via ZInput (decomp/Chat.cs:217). ChatWindowController's legacy block is a duplicate, not the only path. The genuinely dead paths are Escape-closes-compendium (AugaCompendiumController.cs:153 is the only handler; nothing else covers it) and Enter/NumpadEnter submit in GuiInputFieldSubmit/InputFieldSubmitEvents. (2) The decomp grep leaves four legacy call sites, not two: decomp_assembly_utils/InputDefinition.cs:152 and :175 are `Input.GetAxis`, :154 and :173 are `Input.GetKey`. Whether the calls throw or silently return false cannot be settled from disk — globalgamemanagers is binary with no field names and boot.config carries no input setting.
- **Verifier's better fix:** Same replacements, but prioritise AugaCompendiumController.cs:153 and the two submit handlers — those are the only sites with no vanilla fallback. The ChatWindowController close block should just be deleted rather than ported.

### [crafting-8] Auga's Craft/Upgrade tab buttons never read vanilla's forced tab state back, so a station with m_hasCraftTab = false shows the upgrade list under Auga's "Craft" tab

- **Verdict:** partly (reviewer confidence 58)
- **Auga:** AugaUnityLib/AugaCraftingPanel.cs:88-113 (OnTabChanged calls OnTabCraftPressed/OnTabUpgradePressed and never re-reads InCraftTab()/InUpradeTab() afterwards) together with AugaUnityLib/WorkbenchPanelController.cs:84-95 (`WorkbenchContent.GetComponent<AugaTabController>().SelectTab(0)` unconditionally on every OnEnable)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** InventoryGui.UpdateCraftingPanel (decomp/InventoryGui.cs:1125-1131) and InCraftTab/InUpradeTab (decomp/InventoryGui.cs:2543-2559)
- **Trigger:** Walk up to a 1.0 crafting station whose CraftingStation.m_hasCraftTab is false (the dedicated upgrade station) and open the inventory.
- **Consequence:** 1.0 added a branch that force-selects the upgrade tab for such a station (`m_tabCraft.interactable = true; m_tabCraft.gameObject.SetActive(false); m_tabUpgrade.interactable = false; m_tabUpgrade.gameObject.SetActive(true);`), and InCraftTab() additionally now requires `m_tabCraft.gameObject.activeSelf`. Auga's WorkbenchPanelController always selects its tab index 0 on open, which calls OnTabCraftPressed(); vanilla's UpdateCraftingPanel immediately overrides the flags back to upgrade, so UpdateRecipeList builds the upgrade list while Auga's tab strip highlights 'Craft' and shows the craft-side CraftingRequirementsPanel. Clicking Auga's Upgrade tab yields the same list, so the two tabs become indistinguishable.
- **Evidence:** New in 1.0 (auga_audit/diffs/CraftingStation.diff): `public bool m_hasCraftTab = true;`, and in UpdateCraftingPanel the branch `else if ((bool)currentCraftingStation && !currentCraftingStation.m_hasCraftTab)`. Predicate change (auga_audit/diffs/InventoryGui.diff): `-return !((Selectable)m_tabCraft).interactable;` / `+if (!m_tabCraft.interactable) { return m_tabCraft.gameObject.activeSelf; } return false;`. Grep of the Auga tree for m_hasCraftTab: no hits. I could not confirm from the decompile which shipped station prefab sets it to false, so the trigger is inferred from the field's existence and its `= true` default.
- **Fix:** In WorkbenchPanelController.OnEnable pick the tab from the station instead of hardcoding 0: `var s = player.GetCurrentCraftingStation(); WorkbenchContent.GetComponent<AugaTabController>().SelectTab(s != null && !s.m_hasCraftTab ? 1 : 0);` and hide the Craft tab button when `!s.m_hasCraftTab`. Also, after each `UpdateCraftingPanel()` call in AugaCraftingPanel.OnTabChanged, re-sync the visual tab from `InventoryGui.instance.InCraftTab()` / `InUpradeTab()`.
- **Verifier correction:** The code mechanism is exactly as described, but the trigger is unproven: nothing in the supplied materials shows a shipped 1.0 station with m_hasCraftTab = false. The field exists (decomp/CraftingStation.cs:36 `public bool m_hasCraftTab = true;`, new per CraftingStation.diff:29) and the branch exists (decomp/InventoryGui.cs:1125-1131), but the audit has no game prefabs, so whether any station sets it false is unverifiable here. Also note AugaTabController.SelectTab early-returns when SelectedIndex already equals the requested index, so WorkbenchPanelController's SelectTab(0) is a no-op on re-open and OnTabCraftPressed is not re-fired every time.
- **Verifier's better fix:** The fix is sound; add that AugaTabController.SelectTab returns early when SelectedIndex == index, so a re-sync must be done unconditionally (e.g. in AugaCraftingPanel.Update or right after each UpdateCraftingPanel()) rather than relying on SelectTab firing OnTabChanged.

### [widgets-6] 1.0 TabHandler moved the Tab-key check out of the m_gamepadInput guard and defaults m_tabKeyInput to true, so Auga's 2023-serialized TabHandlers now cycle tabs on every Tab press

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 58)
- **Auga:** AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\Compendium.prefab:288-301 (TabHandler, `m_gamepadInput: 0`, no `m_tabKeyInput`/`m_cycling`/`m_blockingElements`/`m_keybaordInput` keys), AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\MainMenu\NewCharacterPanel.prefab:1641-1661, AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\MainMenu.prefab:5918-5931, AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\AugaSettings.prefab:6279-6299
- **Vanilla 1.0:** TabHandler.Update and TabHandler.Init/Start (scratchpad\decomp\TabHandler.cs; diff in auga_audit\diffs\TabHandler.diff)
- **Trigger:** Pressing Tab while an Auga screen that owns a TabHandler is open (compendium, character-creation panel).
- **Consequence:** The tab cycles (`num = 1` -> `SetActiveTabViaButtonIfAvailable`, which invokes the tab button's onClick) on a key the Auga screens never bound. Secondary: `Init(forceSelect: true)` moved from `Awake` to `Start`, and it is now gated by `if (!m_activeTabEverSet && num >= 0)`, so any Auga code that sets a tab before Start, or calls `Init()` again after rebuilding the tab list, will no longer get the `m_default` tab reselected.
- **Evidence:** OLD (decomp_old_assembly_valheim\TabHandler.cs) had the Tab check nested inside the gamepad guard: `if (m_gamepadInput && (gamePad == null || !gamePad.IsBlocked())) { ... if (ZInput.GetKeyDown((KeyCode)9)) { SetActiveTab(...); } }`. NEW: the `m_gamepadInput` block only sets `num`, and then at top level `if (m_tabKeyInput && ZInput.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Tab)) { num = 1; }`. New fields with C# initializers `public bool m_cycling = true;`, `public bool m_tabKeyInput = true;`, `public bool m_keybaordInput;`, `private bool m_activeTabEverSet;`, `public List<GameObject> m_blockingElements = new List<GameObject>();` and `private void Awake()` -> `private void Start()`. Auga's bundle was serialized against the 2023 class, so none of those keys exist in the YAML (confirmed by grep: the four prefabs above contain `m_gamepadInput`/`m_gamepadNavigateLeft`/`m_gamepadNavigateRight`/`m_tabs`/`m_setActiveTabEffects` only) and Unity's type-tree transfer leaves absent fields at their constructor value - i.e. `m_tabKeyInput = true`, `m_cycling = true`, `m_blockingElements` empty (so the new blocking guard is inert too). auga_audit\auga_field_diff.csv: `"TabHandler","5","11","0","6","","m_cycling, m_tabKeyInput, m_keybaordInput, m_keyboardNavigateLeft, m_keyboardNavigateRight, m_blockingElements"`.
- **Fix:** After instantiating each Auga screen, set the new TabHandler fields explicitly (`m_tabKeyInput = false` unless the screen wants Tab cycling, `m_cycling` to taste, and populate `m_blockingElements` with the screen's modal children), or add them to the PortCarryOver default-application table from the previous finding. If Auga wants Tab cycling in the compendium, also populate `m_blockingElements` so it does not fire while a sub-dialog is up.

### [main-menu-3] AugaPassword.prefab and AugaConnecting.prefab have no UIGroupHandler, unlike every other Auga dialog prefab

- **Verdict:** partly (reviewer confidence 45)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\AugaPassword.prefab and AugaConnecting.prefab (roots carry only RectTransform, CanvasRenderer, Localize)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** UIGamePad.IsInteractive / UIGamePad.Update (decomp/UIGamePad.cs:36-76) and UIGroupHandler (decomp_assembly_guiutils/UIGroupHandler.cs)
- **Trigger:** Joining a password-protected server while playing on a gamepad.
- **Consequence:** 1.0's UIGamePad resolves its group with `m_group = GetComponentInParent<UIGroupHandler>()` and returns false from IsInteractive() when that group is not active; with no UIGroupHandler on Auga's dialog the nearest one is whatever scene ancestor exists, which is not activated when the dialog opens. Expected effect: the gamepad hint on the password field never appears, the dialog never takes gamepad focus/default element, and it does not raise group priority to block the UI behind it.
- **Evidence:** Searching every Auga prefab for UIGroupHandler (fileID -626263950, guid d920e4723ba2566674efb2f0c3eb6edb, MD4-verified) matches AugaMenu, AugaSettings, Compendium, ConfirmDialog, ContainerPanel, Credits, InfoPanel, Inventory_screen, SimpleDialog, SplitDialog, MainMenu*, PlayerPanel, RightPanel - but NOT AugaPassword.prefab or AugaConnecting.prefab. AugaPassword's only game components are `Localize` (fileID 182242463, guiutils) on the root and `UIGamePad` (fileID 702673311, m_zinputKey: JoyRStick) on the TextFieldLegacy instance. 1.0 also added `m_availableForConsoleMouse` to UIGamePad, which is a bool and therefore unfillable by PortCarryOver even if a donor existed (it defaults false, so IsBlocked() returns true whenever ZInput.IsGamepadMouseActive()).
- **Fix:** Add a UIGroupHandler (with m_defaultElement pointing at the TextFieldLegacy and a high m_groupPriority) to AugaPassword.prefab, and mirror whatever the vanilla dialog carries. Capture ZNet_vanilla.txt first (finding 2) to see the real component set before guessing.
- **Verifier correction:** The structural observation is true and I reproduced it: searching for UIGroupHandler (fileID -626263950, guid d920e4723ba2566674efb2f0c3eb6edb, map-verified) across AugaUnity/Assets matches exactly 16 prefabs (AugaMenu, AugaSettings, Compendium, ConfirmDialog, ContainerPanel, Credits, InfoPanel, Inventory_screen, MainMenu, NewCharacterPanel, PlayerPanel, RightPanel, SelectCharacter, SimpleDialog, SplitDialog, StartGame_old) and NOT AugaPassword.prefab or AugaConnecting.prefab; AugaPassword carries Localize (182242463) and UIGamePad (702673311) only. But the causal story is wrong on two counts. (1) This is not a 1.0 regression: decomp_old_assembly_valheim/UIGamePad.cs:24/37/68 has the identical `private UIGroupHandler m_group;`, `m_group = GetComponentInParent<UIGroupHandler>()` in Start and `if (m_group && !m_group.IsActive) return false;` in IsInteractive - byte-for-byte the same mechanism Auga shipped against in 2023. (2) The stated effect does not follow: decomp/UIGamePad.cs:68-71 guards with `if ((bool)m_group && ...)`, so when there is NO UIGroupHandler ancestor at all, m_group is null and IsInteractive returns TRUE, not false. The only genuinely new 1.0 hazard on these prefabs is `public bool m_availableForConsoleMouse;` (decomp/UIGamePad.cs:13), which the new IsBlocked (decomp/UIGamePad.cs:146-156) ANDs in as `(m_availableForConsoleMouse || !ZInput.IsGamepadMouseActive())` where the old body (decomp_old_assembly_valheim/UIGamePad.cs) had no such term - and being a bool it is invisible to PortCarryOver.
- **Verifier's better fix:** Drop the 1.0 framing. If the gamepad hint/focus really is broken on these dialogs, capture the vanilla ZNet password/connecting hierarchy first and mirror its component set; the concretely new 1.0 gap to close is m_availableForConsoleMouse, which defaults false on every Auga UIGamePad in the bundle (36 of them) and is a bool PortCarryOver structurally cannot fill - set it in code for the dialogs that must stay usable under ZInput.IsGamepadMouseActive().

## Severity: missing

### [main-menu-2] ZNet is in neither PortDiagnostics.TargetMethods() nor PortHierarchyDump.TargetMethods(), so the one live swap in this area has zero coverage

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 95)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\PortDiagnostics.cs:23-36 and PortHierarchyDump.cs:22-37
- **Vanilla 1.0:** ZNet.Awake (decomp/ZNet.cs:333)
- **Trigger:** Every game start with [Debug] PortDiagnostics = true.
- **Consequence:** No `ZNet_vanilla.txt` is ever written, so nobody can see what the untouched 1.0 PasswordDialog/ConnectingDialog contained, and no "N destroyed, N unassigned" line is emitted for ZNet. The two dialogs Auga replaces are the only replaced UI in the build with no diagnostic at all - which is exactly why finding 1 and finding 3 cannot be settled from the materials on disk.
- **Evidence:** PortDiagnostics.TargetMethods() yields Hud, InventoryGui, Minimap, Menu, FejdStartup, Chat, StoreGui, TextInput, EnemyHud, MessageHud, DamageText - no ZNet. PortHierarchyDump.TargetMethods() yields the same set plus TextViewer and PlayerCustomizaton - no ZNet. The dumps folder confirms it: auga_audit\dumps\ contains 13 *_vanilla.txt files and none is ZNet. Run logs auga_run1..4 contain no `[PortDiagnostics] ZNet` line.
- **Fix:** Add `yield return AccessTools.Method(typeof(ZNet), "Awake");` to both TargetMethods() lists. PortHierarchyDump's Priority.First prefix will then write ZNet_vanilla.txt before Connection_Setup's postfix destroys anything.

### [main-menu-6] Every text element in Auga's main-menu prefabs is legacy UnityEngine.UI.Text while 1.0 FejdStartup declares all of them TMP_Text

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 95)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\MainMenu.prefab (28 UI.Text refs), MainMenu\SelectCharacter.prefab (5), MainMenu\NewCharacterPanel.prefab (7), Credits.prefab (19), MainMenu\WorldListElement.prefab (2), MainMenu\ServerListElement.prefab (3) - 0 TextMeshProUGUI refs in any of them; the assignments are MainMenu_Setup.cs:46, 57, 73, 80, 130, 133, 150, 186, 187
- **Vanilla 1.0:** FejdStartup field declarations (decomp/FejdStartup.cs:41, 49, 195, 199, 204, 208, 210, 212) and FejdStartup.UpdateCharacterList (decomp/FejdStartup.cs:2711)
- **Trigger:** Re-enabling MainMenu_Setup's block.
- **Consequence:** The whole block fails to compile: `m_versionLabel`, `m_connectionFailedError`, `m_passwordError`, `m_removeWorldName`, `m_removeCharacterName`, `m_csName`, `m_csFileSource`, `m_csSourceInfo` are `TMP_Text` in 1.0 but MainMenu_Setup assigns `GetComponent<Text>()`; `m_serverPassword`, `m_newWorldName`, `m_newWorldSeed`, `m_csNewCharacterName` are `GuiInputField` but MainMenu_Setup assigns `GetComponent<InputField>()`. Even after the C# is retyped, the prefabs supply no TMP components for the labels, so the fields would be null.
- **Evidence:** Counts obtained by grepping for guid 5f7201a12d95ffc409449d95f23cf332 (UnityEngine.UI.Text) vs f4688fdb7df04437aeb418b961361dc5 (TextMeshProUGUI) per prefab. There is no `UnityEngine.UI.InputField` (guid d199490a83bb2b844b9695cbf13b01ef) anywhere under AugaUnity\Assets - MainMenu.prefab (165 refs) and NewCharacterPanel.prefab (31 refs) nest TextField.prefab, which already carries GUIFramework.GuiInputField after the port's fileID rewrite, so only the text labels and the C# need work. This is pre-existing debt, not a 1.0 regression: 0.217.30 already declared the same fields TMP_Text/GuiInputField (decomp_old_assembly_valheim/FejdStartup.cs:51, 59, 127, 129, 154, 156, 166, 170, 195-201).
- **Fix:** Convert the labels in MainMenu.prefab / SelectCharacter.prefab / NewCharacterPanel.prefab / Credits.prefab / the two list elements to TextMeshProUGUI, then retype the assignments in MainMenu_Setup to `GetComponent<TMP_Text>()` and `GetComponent<GuiInputField>()` before uncommenting.

### [pause-texts-8] TextsDialog.UpdateTextsList prefix returns false, so 1.0's new AddStats() page ($inventory_stats) never exists under Auga

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 93)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\PauseMenu_Setup.cs:288-308 (TextsDialog_Patch.UpdateTextsList_Prefix returns false)

Auga's replacement list only contains the player's known texts. AddStats() is also the only caller of its ZLog dump, so the log line is lost too.

Auga patches AddLog and AddActiveEffects with explicit `return false` prefixes (PauseMenu_Setup.cs:274-286) but AddStats is not patched at all - it simply never runs because UpdateTextsList_Prefix short-circuits the caller.

### [hud-build-3] 1.0's new build-menu key hints (m_buildMenuHintsKB/GP) land on Auga's hidden KeyHints donor, so 'Close Build Menu' and 'Favorite' are never shown

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 92) - crash already prevented by PortCarryOver
- **Auga:** Auga/Hud_Setup.cs:201 (`__instance.transform.Replace("hudroot/KeyHints", Auga.Assets.Hud)`) + the serialized KeyHints copy in AugaUnity/Assets/Prefabs/HUD.prefab:19074-19107 (22 fields, no m_buildMenuHintsKB/m_buildMenuHintsGP)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** KeyHints.UpdateHints (scratchpad/decomp/KeyHints.cs:214-234), fields declared at decomp/KeyHints.cs:34-36
- **Trigger:** Player enters place mode with a build tool and opens the build menu.
- **Consequence:** No exception (PortCarryOver fills both arrays), but every element they toggle lives under the inactive `hudroot/KeyHints_VanillaDonor`, so `array[j].SetActive(true)` shows nothing. The 1.0 build-menu hints are silently absent from Auga's hint bar.
- **Evidence:** 1.0 added `public GameObject[] m_buildMenuHintsKB;` and `public GameObject[] m_buildMenuHintsGP;` (decomp/KeyHints.cs:34-36) and dereferences them without a null check: `GameObject[] array = (ZInput.IsGamepadActive() ? m_buildMenuHintsGP : m_buildMenuHintsKB); for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++) array[j].SetActive(value: true);` (decomp/KeyHints.cs:216-220) plus the matching hide branch at :224-233. auga_field_diff.csv row `"KeyHints","22","22","3","3",...,"m_buildMenuHintsKB, m_buildMenuHintsGP, m_radialKeyHints"` confirms Auga's prefab lacks them. run4 log line 253 proves the carry-over fired: `[PortDiagnostics] KeyHints on 'KeyHints(Clone)': 5 empty references filled from the hidden vanilla object: m_equipButtons[], m_buildMenuHintsKB[], m_buildMenuHintsGP[], m_cycleSnapKey, m_radialKeyHints`. The donor objects are `hudroot/KeyHints/BuildHints/Keyboard/Close Build Menu` and `.../Favorite` plus `BuildHints/Gamepad/Text - Close Build Menu` and `Text - Favorite` (dumps/Hud_vanilla.txt:370-371, 384-391).
- **Fix:** Add 'Close Build Menu' and 'Favorite' hint rows to Auga's BuildHints Keyboard and Gamepad groups in HUD.prefab and assign them to `m_buildMenuHintsKB` / `m_buildMenuHintsGP`, so the carry-over no longer needs to fill them with donor objects.

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 92) - crash already prevented by PortCarryOver
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\AugaMenu.prefab:449 (`m_gamepadRoot: {fileID: 0}`); no GamepadMapController anywhere in the bundle. Filled by Auga\PortCarryOver.cs:104-110 via Auga\PauseMenu_Setup.cs:251-252.

- **Trigger:** Open the pause menu with a gamepad connected/active.

The whole 1.0 gamepad control-map overlay is invisible; gamepad players get no button legend in the pause menu. (Currently masked because Show() throws earlier at SetButtonsEnabled, but it will surface the moment that is fixed.)

- **Fix:** Reparent the donor's `MenuRoot/GamepadMap` subtree under the Auga menu root (or copy it) in Menu_Start_Patch.Postfix and assign `newMenu.m_gamepadRoot` / `newMenu.m_gamepadMapController` to the moved copies; alternatively give AugaMenu.prefab its own gamepad-map holder and assign m_gamepadMapController at runtime from the donor's component after reparenting.

### [hud-bars-2] Hud.m_loadingIndicator still points into the hidden LoadingBlack_VanillaDonor; Auga's LoadingBlack has no LoadingIndicator, so 1.0's world-generation progress is never visible

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 88) - crash already prevented by PortCarryOver
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\Hud_Setup.cs:49-56 — `var loadingScreen = __instance.Replace("LoadingBlack", Auga.Assets.Hud);` then reassigns m_loadingScreen, m_loadingProgress, m_sleepingProgress, m_teleportingProgress, m_loadingImage, m_loadingTip — m_loadingIndicator is not reassigned. AugaUnity/Assets/Prefabs/HUD.prefab: LoadingBlack/Loading has only Image, TopFade, BottomFade, text_darken, Tip.
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Hud.m_loadingIndicator (decomp/Hud.cs:223) consumed by Hud.UpdateProgressIndicator (decomp/Hud.cs:684-701: `m_loadingIndicator.SetProgress(ZoneSystem.instance.GenerateLocationsProgress)`, `SetShowProgress(flag)`, `SetText("$menu_generating")`), called from Hud.UpdateBlackScreen when `Game.instance.WaitingForRespawn() && !CinematicsManager.IsPlaying()`
- **Trigger:** Hosting or playing singleplayer on a world whose locations are not yet generated — i.e. the black loading screen on first entry to a new world (UpdateProgressIndicator early-returns unless `ZNet.instance.IsServer()`).
- **Consequence:** No exception (the donor component is alive, just parented under an inactive GameObject). SetProgress/SetShowProgress/SetText all run against a hidden object, so the 1.0 "$menu_generating" spinner and generation progress bar never appear — the player sees only Auga's loading art and a tip, with no indication the world is still generating.
- **Evidence:** auga_audit/dumps/Hud_vanilla.txt:777-782 shows the new 1.0 object `LoadingBlack/Loading/LoadingIndicator  [LoadingIndicator]` with children Center/Background, Center/Spinner, Center/ProgressIndicator, Center/LoadingText. Auga's HUD.prefab tree under HUD/LoadingBlack/Loading contains Image, TopFade, BottomFade, text_darken/Logotype, Tip and nothing named LoadingIndicator (LoadingIndicator is a 1.0-only class, so a 2023 bundle cannot contain it). AugaExtensions.Replace (Auga/Extensions.cs:84-90) calls PortCarryOver.MakeDonor, which does `original.SetActive(false); original.name += "_VanillaDonor"`, so the vanilla branch survives hidden. m_loadingIndicator is absent from the `[PortDiagnostics] Hud: ... 15 unassigned` list (auga_run4.log:255), proving it is non-null and therefore still bound to the donor's component.
- **Fix:** Either add a LoadingIndicator-bearing child to Auga's LoadingBlack/Loading and assign `__instance.m_loadingIndicator = loadingScreen.Find("Loading/LoadingIndicator").GetComponent<LoadingIndicator>();` alongside the m_loadingTip assignment at Hud_Setup.cs:55, or reparent the donor's `Loading/LoadingIndicator` transform into Auga's `Loading` before hiding the donor (as is already done for m_dreamTexts at lines 48/56) and keep the existing field value.
- **Verifier's better fix:** Prefer the second option (reparent the donor's `Loading/LoadingIndicator` into Auga's `Loading`, as is already done for m_dreamTexts at Hud_Setup.cs:48/56) — it needs no new prefab work. One correction to the finding's metadata: `covered_by_carryover` should be false. PortCarryOver.FillComponent only fills NULL Object references (PortCarryOver.cs:100-108), and the Hud component is not on the replaced LoadingBlack root at all, so carry-over never inspects it; the field is non-null purely because the vanilla scene reference survived.

### [minimap-2] The 1.0 Ping pin type is unreachable: Auga's large map prefab has no IconPingPanel, so m_selectedIconPing / m_pingImageObject / m_touchPingPanel all resolve into the hidden large_VanillaDonor

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 88) - crash already prevented by PortCarryOver
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\Minimap_Setup.cs:115-129 (assigns m_selectedIcon0..4/Boss/Death and rebuilds m_selectedIcons, never m_selectedIconPing) and :156-170 (no listener for OnPressedPingIcon); prefab AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\HUD.prefab, object HUD/hudroot/MiniMap/large — children are MapBG, large_map, PublicPanel, SharedPanel, PingPanel, IconPanel, IconDeath, IconBoss, biome, GamepadCrosshair; no IconPingPanel
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Minimap.SelectIcon / Minimap.OnPressedPingIcon / Minimap.OnEnable / Minimap.TouchLayoutChanged / Minimap.Start (scratchpad\decomp\Minimap.cs:2615-2624, :2534, :462-468, :1200-1207, :568)
- **Trigger:** Open the large map and look for the ping-pin selector next to the pin icons; or (touch) expect the ping panel to appear when the touch layout activates.
- **Consequence:** PinType.Ping can never be selected, so the 1.0 feature "double-click the map to send a ping to your group" (Minimap.OnMapDblClick -> Chat.instance.SendPing) is dead under Auga — only middle-click and the gamepad JoyButtonX path remain. m_pingImageObject.sprite = m_pingIcon and m_selectedIconPing.enabled = ... both operate on Images inside the inactive large_VanillaDonor, so they render nowhere. No exception.
- **Evidence:** 1.0 added `public Image m_selectedIconPing; public Transform m_touchPingPanel; public Sprite m_pingIcon; public Sprite m_pingIconMac; public Image m_pingImageObject;` (Minimap.diff @@ -198,10 +190,20 @@; auga_field_diff.csv row "Minimap","55","63","0","8" lists exactly m_selectedIconPing, m_touchPingPanel, m_pingIcon, m_pingIconMac, m_pingImageObject, m_touchVibrationPrefab, m_longPressToDeleteTime, m_mapTexture as new). SelectIcon now starts `m_selectedIconPing.enabled = type == PinType.Ping;`. The 1.0 hierarchy dump has `large/IconPingPanel [Image]`, `large/IconPingPanel/IconPing [Image, Button, MouseClick]`, `large/IconPingPanel/IconPing/Selected [Image]` (dumps\Minimap_vanilla.txt:42-46); Auga's prefab has no object of any of those names. Run4's `[PortDiagnostics] Minimap: 0 destroyed, 0 unassigned` proves the fields are non-null, i.e. still pointing at the donor.
- **Fix:** Add an IconPingPanel/IconPing (+ Selected child) to HUD/hudroot/MiniMap/large in AugaUnity, then in Minimap_Start_Postfix add `minimap.m_selectedIconPing = newMap.Find("IconPingPanel/IconPing/Selected").GetComponent<Image>(); minimap.m_pingImageObject = newMap.Find("IconPingPanel/IconPing").GetComponent<Image>(); minimap.m_touchPingPanel = newMap.Find("IconPingPanel");` plus `SetButtonListener(newMap.transform, "IconPingPanel/IconPing", minimap.OnPressedPingIcon);`. Until the prefab exists, at minimum point m_selectedIconPing at a hidden stand-in Image on Auga's own large root so nothing depends on the donor.

### [auga-lib-4] ComplexTooltip never calls Player.AppendEquipmentModifierTooltips, so 1.0's eleven equipment modifiers (including trinkets' max-adrenaline) are missing from every item tooltip

- **Verdict:** partly (reviewer confidence 88)
- **Auga:** AugaUnityLib/ComplexTooltip.cs:348-502 (GenerateItemTextBoxes); the modifier block at :460-482 only handles m_eitrRegenModifier and m_movementModifier
- **Vanilla 1.0:** ItemDrop.ItemData.GetTooltip -> Player.AppendEquipmentModifierTooltips (decomp/ItemDrop.cs:1023-1026, decomp/Player.cs:2594-2614, modifier table at decomp/Player.cs:136-146)
- **Trigger:** Hover any armour piece, utility item or Trinket (ItemType 24, new in 1.0) that carries one of the new modifier fields.
- **Consequence:** Ten of the eleven modifiers 1.0 prints are invisible under Auga: $base_item_modifier, $item_heat_modifier, $se_jumpstamina, $se_attackstamina, $se_blockstamina, $se_dodgestamina, $se_swimstamina, $se_sneakstamina, $se_runstamina and $item_maxadrenaline. Since ComplexTooltip's switch also has no case for ItemType.Trinket, a trinket whose only stats are these modifiers shows a tooltip with nothing but value/quality/weight. The GetExtraTextFromTooltip fallback does not reliably rescue them: it only starts emitting after the first blank line following $item_weight, and AppendEquipmentModifierTooltips writes plain '\n$...' lines with no blank line before them, so on items with no status-effect or chain tooltip the lines are dropped by the `if (!foundFirstColor) continue;` filter (ComplexTooltip.cs:525-529).
- **Evidence:** 1.0 adds `if (player != null) { player.AppendEquipmentModifierTooltips(item, m_stringBuilder); }` to GetTooltip (decomp/ItemDrop.cs:1023-1026) driven by `s_equipmentModifierSources = { "m_movementModifier", "m_homeItemsStaminaModifier", "m_heatResistanceModifier", "m_jumpStaminaModifier", "m_attackStaminaModifier", "m_blockStaminaModifier", "m_dodgeStaminaModifier", "m_swimStaminaModifier", "m_sneakStaminaModifier", "m_runStaminaModifier", "m_maxAdrenaline" }` (decomp/Player.cs:136-140). Every one of those SharedData fields except m_movementModifier returns 0 hits in decomp_old_assembly_valheim/ItemDrop.cs, and `AppendEquipmentModifierTooltips` returns 0 hits in the old Player.cs. `Trinket = 24` is new in the ItemType enum (decomp/ItemDrop.cs:36 vs decomp_old_assembly_valheim/ItemDrop.cs:35 which ends at AmmoNonEquipable = 23).
- **Fix:** In GenerateItemTextBoxes, after the teleport/movement/eitr box, build a StringBuilder, call `Player.m_localPlayer.AppendEquipmentModifierTooltips(item, sb)` and emit each non-empty line through TextBoxAddPreprocessedLine on a TwoColumnTextBoxPrefab (splitting on ':' for the label/value columns). Add `case ItemDrop.ItemData.ItemType.Trinket:` to the switch so trinkets at least get that box.
- **Verifier correction:** The structural core is right: AugaUnityLib/ComplexTooltip.cs never calls Player.AppendEquipmentModifierTooltips, its modifier block (:460-482) handles only m_eitrRegenModifier and m_movementModifier, and `grep -rn Trinket --include=*.cs` over the whole Auga tree returns ZERO hits, so ItemType.Trinket (decomp/ItemDrop.cs:36, new - old enum ends at AmmoNonEquipable = 23) has no case in GenerateItemTextBoxes. But 'ten of the eleven are invisible' is too strong, and the reason given for the fallback failing is wrong. ComplexTooltip.cs:230 sets `_originalTooltip = item.GetTooltip();`, which routes through decomp/ItemDrop.cs:793-795 -> the static GetTooltip, so AppendEquipmentModifierTooltips output IS in _originalTooltip. GetExtraTextFromTooltip (ComplexTooltip.cs:504-539) does not need a blank line: it sets foundFirstColor on any post-weight line that starts with '<color' OR does not start with '$item_'/'$inventory_'. The seven `$se_*` keys and `$base_item_modifier` satisfy that second test, so they turn the filter on and are emitted, along with everything after them. Only `$item_movement_modifier`, `$item_heat_modifier` and `$item_maxadrenaline` are dropped, and only while no earlier non-$item_ line has appeared - e.g. a trinket whose sole stat is $item_maxadrenaline. So the real defect is that these modifiers land in an unformatted left-aligned dump instead of a stat box, and a handful are silently lost.
- **Verifier's better fix:** Keep the proposed AppendEquipmentModifierTooltips + TwoColumnTextBox approach and the `case ItemDrop.ItemData.ItemType.Trinket:` addition, but also suppress the duplicate: once the modifiers are emitted as their own box they will ALSO still come through GetExtraTextFromTooltip for the $se_*/$base_ keys, so strip those lines from the extra-text fallback (or set showExtraText false for them) to avoid printing each modifier twice.

### [auga-lib-8] AugaHealthBar has no Adrenaline mode, so 1.0's new adrenaline bar has no Auga equivalent

- **Verdict:** partly (reviewer confidence 86)
- **Auga:** AugaUnityLib/AugaHealthBar.cs:11 (`public enum ModeType { Health, Stamina, Eitr }`) and the LateUpdate switch at :59-78
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Hud.UpdateAdrenaline and the Adrenaline header block (decomp/Hud.cs:179-188, 534, 998-1002, 1122-1140); Player.GetAdrenaline / GetMaxAdrenaline (decomp/Player.cs:348, decomp/Character.cs GetMaxAdrenaline)
- **Trigger:** Play with an item or status effect that grants adrenaline (a combat-heavy 1.0 session); the vanilla HUD would show an adrenaline bar above stamina.
- **Consequence:** No adrenaline readout anywhere in the Auga HUD — the player cannot see the resource at all, and the stat is used by the new adrenaline status-effect levels and $item_maxadrenaline trinkets.
- **Evidence:** Adrenaline is entirely new: `grep -i adrenaline decomp/Hud.cs` returns the whole `[Header("Adrenaline")] public RectTransform m_adrenalineBarRoot; public Animator m_adrenalineAnimator; public GuiBar m_adrenalineBarFast; public GuiBar m_adrenalineBarSlow; public TMP_Text m_adrenalineText;` block plus `UpdateAdrenaline(localPlayer, deltaTime)` at line 534, while the same grep over decomp_old_assembly_valheim/Hud.cs returns nothing. Auga's stat bar only knows Health/Stamina/Eitr and calls GetHealth/GetStamina/GetEitr accordingly (AugaHealthBar.cs:59-78). PORT_1.0.md already lists 'adrenaline bar' under 'new 1.0 HUD parts have no Auga prefab yet'.
- **Fix:** Add `Adrenaline` to AugaHealthBar.ModeType with `CurrentValue = player.GetAdrenaline(); MaxValue = player.GetMaxAdrenaline(); MaxPotentialValue = player.GetMaxAdrenaline();`, add a bar instance to the Auga HUD prefab, and point Hud.m_adrenalineBarFast/Slow/Root/Text/Animator at it in Hud_Setup so 1.0's UpdateAdrenaline has non-null targets.
- **Verifier correction:** The Auga-side facts are right: AugaUnityLib/AugaHealthBar.cs:11 is `public enum ModeType { Health, Stamina, Eitr };` and the LateUpdate switch (:59-78) only handles those three, and PORT_1.0.md:54 lists 'adrenaline bar' among the new 1.0 HUD parts with no Auga prefab. The consequence is wrong. Vanilla's adrenaline UI lives at `hudroot/adrenalinepanel` (dumps/Hud_vanilla.txt:15-24: adrenalinepanel [Animator], Stamina/adrenaline_slow [GuiBar], adrenaline_fast [GuiBar], adrenalineText [TextMeshProUGUI]) - a SIBLING of hudroot/healthpanel, not a child. Auga/Hud_Setup.cs only destroys `__instance.m_healthPanel`'s children (:90-93), m_staminaBar2Root (:94) and m_eitrBarRoot (:95), and `grep -rn adrenalinepanel|adrenaline` over all Auga .cs returns nothing - the panel is untouched. Hud.UpdateAdrenaline is not patched either: Hud_Setup.cs patches only Awake, UpdateStatusEffects, UpdateFood, SetHealthBarSize, SetStaminaBarSize, UpdateHealth, UpdateStamina, UpdateEitr. And auga_run4.log:256's 15 unassigned Hud fields are all health/food/stamina/eitr - m_adrenalineBarRoot/Fast/Slow/Text/Animator are NOT among them. So 1.0's UpdateAdrenaline already has non-null targets and vanilla's adrenaline bar still updates and renders; the real defect is that it renders in vanilla styling at vanilla coordinates, clashing with Auga's relocated MovableHudElement bars, with no Auga equivalent.
- **Verifier's better fix:** Add the Adrenaline ModeType and an Auga bar as proposed, but the Hud field re-pointing is not about avoiding nulls - it is about replacing a still-live vanilla widget. Re-point m_adrenalineBarRoot/Fast/Slow/Text/Animator at the new Auga bar AND dispose of `hudroot/adrenalinepanel` the way the other panels are handled (Replace via PortCarryOver.MakeDonor, or destroy its children as Hud_Setup.cs:90-93 does for m_healthPanel), otherwise the vanilla bar keeps drawing alongside the new one.

### [inventory-5] InventoryGrid.m_ensureVisible (new in 1.0) is null on both Auga grids; the container grid also lacks ScrollRect/ScrollRectEnsureVisible

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 85)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\PlayerPanel.prefab:619-628 and ContainerPanel.prefab:1803-1812 (InventoryGrid, m_Script fileID 1878785250 — serialized keys are only m_tooltipAnchor, m_elementPrefab, m_gridRoot, m_scrollbar, m_uiGroup, m_elementSpace)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** InventoryGrid.UpdateGamepad — scratchpad\decomp\InventoryGrid.cs:164-172 (`if (m_ensureVisible != null) m_ensureVisible.CenterOnItem(gamepadSelectedElement);`)
- **Trigger:** Gamepad: moving the selection down a container whose contents are taller than the visible panel.
- **Consequence:** No exception (the call is null-guarded), but the scroll view never follows the gamepad selection, so the selected slot can move off-screen with no way to see it. Vanilla 1.0 puts ScrollRect + ScrollRectEnsureVisible on the container grid for exactly this.
- **Evidence:** `public ScrollRectEnsureVisible m_ensureVisible;` is one of the fields added in 1.0 (auga_audit\diffs\InventoryGrid.diff `+ public ScrollRectEnsureVisible m_ensureVisible;`; auga_field_diff.csv row InventoryGrid lists m_ensureVisible under New). Neither Auga InventoryGrid block contains an `m_ensureVisible:` key, so it deserializes null, and `grep -rn m_ensureVisible --include=*.cs` over the Auga tree returns nothing. auga_audit\dumps\InventoryGui_vanilla.txt line 37: `root/Player/Container/ContainerGrid [InventoryGrid, RectMask2D, ScrollRect, ScrollRectEnsureVisible]` versus Auga's ContainerPanel/ContainerGrid which carries only the InventoryGrid. PortCarryOver cannot fill it: InventoryGrid is not on the donor root that Fill() iterates (PortCarryOver.cs:62).
- **Fix:** Add a ScrollRectEnsureVisible (and the ScrollRect it needs) to ContainerPanel/ContainerGrid in the Unity project and assign it to the grid's m_ensureVisible; or, at runtime in the Awake postfix, `__instance.m_containerGrid.m_ensureVisible = containerInventory.GetComponentInChildren<ScrollRectEnsureVisible>();` once such a component exists.

### [text-chat-4] Auga's TextViewer Raven style is a stub: m_ravenRoot is 'Dummy/DummyRaven' with no AnimatorController under a parent that is never activated

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 85)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\AugaTextViewer.prefab lines 61-63: `m_ravenRoot: {fileID: 8254342561331129535}` (= AugaTextViewer/Dummy/DummyRaven), `m_ravenTopic: {fileID: 8173622406815899983}` (= Dummy/topic), `m_ravenText: {fileID: 5863612720995732470}` (= Dummy/DummyText). The Animator on DummyRaven (&5662895526664626131, line 1605) has `m_Controller: {fileID: 0}`, and its parent GameObject "Dummy" (&6472783780718418713) has `m_IsActive: 0`.
- **Vanilla 1.0:** TextViewer.Awake / Hide / IsVisible / ShowText case Style.Raven (scratchpad\decomp\TextViewer.cs:56-66, 94-136, 141-152)
- **Trigger:** Every frame: TextViewer.LateUpdate -> IsVisible() -> `m_animatorRaven.GetBool(s_visibleID)`, and Hud.UpdateCrosshair -> `TextViewer.instance.IsVisible()`. Also any Hide().
- **Consequence:** m_animatorRaven is a permanently uninitialised Animator (no controller, GameObject inactive because Awake's `m_ravenRoot.SetActive(true)` only sets DummyRaven's own flag while the inactive parent "Dummy" keeps it out of the hierarchy). Style.Raven would render nothing, and the Raven branch of 1.0's new hover-name gate reads a dead Animator. Vanilla 1.0 has a real `textroot_raven [Animator, CanvasGroup]` with bkg/topic/Text.
- **Evidence:** dumps\TextViewer_vanilla.txt lines 15-18: `textroot_raven  (inactive)  [Animator, CanvasGroup]` + bkg/topic/Text. Auga prefab dump: `AugaTextViewer/Dummy act=0` -> `Dummy/DummyRaven act=1 [RectTransform, Animator#5662895526664626131]` with m_Controller fileID 0. 1.0 `Hide()`: `m_animatorRaven.SetBool(s_visibleID, value: false);`, `IsVisible()`: `return m_animatorRaven.GetBool(s_visibleID);`. Grep of decomp\*.cs for `Style.Raven` finds only the switch case itself, so no 1.0 caller currently uses it - the loss is latent, the dead Animator read is not.
- **Fix:** Build a real raven branch in AugaTextViewer (copy the intro branch: Animator + CanvasGroup + bkg/topic/Text) and point m_ravenRoot/m_ravenTopic/m_ravenText at it; at minimum give DummyRaven the RuneText-style controller with a `visible` bool and reparent it out of the inactive "Dummy" holder so Awake's SetActive(true) actually takes effect.

### [harmony-10] Player.UpdateKnownRecipesList prefix re-scan misses 1.0's seasonal recipes/pieces and lacks vanilla's recipe.m_item null guard

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 85)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\AugaLog_Hooks.cs:75-116 (Player_UpdateKnownRecipesList_Patch.Prefix)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Player.UpdateKnownRecipesList (decomp\Player.cs:5330-5363)
- **Trigger:** Learn a recipe or building piece that is only enabled by the active season (m_currentSeason).
- **Consequence:** Seasonal recipes and seasonal pieces are added to m_knownRecipes by vanilla but never produce an Auga 'new recipe' / 'new piece' log entry. Secondary risk: Auga's copy omits vanilla's `(bool)recipe.m_item` test, so a modded/season ObjectDB entry with a null m_item NREs in the prefix (this predates 1.0 but the recipe set is larger now).
- **Evidence:** 1.0: `bool flag = m_currentSeason != null && m_currentSeason.Recipes.Contains(recipe); if ((recipe.m_enabled || flag) && (bool)recipe.m_item && !m_knownRecipes.Contains(...) && HaveRequirements(recipe, discover: true, 0))` and `bool flag3 = m_currentSeason != null && m_currentSeason.Pieces.Contains(piece); if ((component.m_enabled || flag3) && ...)`. Auga: `if (recipe.m_enabled && !__instance.m_knownRecipes.Contains(recipe.m_item.m_itemData.m_shared.m_name) && __instance.HaveRequirements(recipe, true, 0))` and `if (piece.m_enabled && !__instance.m_knownRecipes.Contains(piece.m_name) && __instance.HaveRequirements(piece, Player.RequirementMode.IsKnown))`.
- **Fix:** Mirror the 1.0 conditions: add the `m_currentSeason` terms and the `recipe.m_item != null` guard. Cheaper alternative: drop the re-scan entirely and hook Player.AddKnownRecipe / Player.AddKnownPiece (both exist in 1.0) as postfixes instead.

### [harmony-11] InventoryGui.DoCrafting prefix's eligibility copy is out of date with 1.0, so several successful crafts produce no Auga log entry

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 85)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\AugaLog_Hooks.cs:161-199 (InventoryGui_DoCrafting_Patch.Prefix)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** InventoryGui.DoCrafting (decomp\InventoryGui.cs:1782ff)
- **Trigger:** Craft at an upgrader station past max quality, craft on a world with the NoCraftCost global key, multicraft more than one, or craft an item that stacks into an already-occupied slot with no free slot.
- **Consequence:** AugaMessageLog.AddCraftItemLog / AddUpgradeItemLog is skipped for those crafts (the item is still crafted - the prefix always returns true, so nothing crashes). Multicrafted amounts are also logged as a single item.
- **Evidence:** 1.0 gates on `if (num > m_craftRecipe.m_item.m_itemData.m_shared.m_maxQuality && !flag)` where `flag = (bool)currentCraftingStation && currentCraftingStation.m_upgrader`; on `!player.HaveRequirements(m_craftRecipe, discover: false, num, num2) && !player.NoCostCheat() && !ZoneSystem.instance.GetGlobalKey(GlobalKeys.NoCraftCost)` with `num2 = m_multiCrafting ? m_multiCraftAmount : 1`; and on `m_craftUpgradeItem == null && !player.GetInventory().CanAddItem(m_craftRecipe.m_item.gameObject, num3)`. Auga tests `quality > maxQuality`, `!player.HaveRequirements(__instance.m_craftRecipe, false, quality) && !player.NoCostCheat()`, and `m_craftUpgradeItem == null && !player.GetInventory().HaveEmptySlot()`.
- **Fix:** Re-sync the copy with 1.0's DoCrafting (add the m_upgrader exemption, the GlobalKeys.NoCraftCost term, pass `m_multiCrafting ? m_multiCraftAmount : 1` to HaveRequirements, and use CanAddItem instead of HaveEmptySlot), or replace the whole thing with a postfix that compares the inventory before/after.
- **Verifier's better fix:** Fix is right; the before/after postfix is the better of the two options. If you re-sync the copy instead, note 1.0 added one more early return the finding does not list: `|| (m_craftRecipe.m_requireOnlyOneIngredient && singleReqItem == null)` at decomp/InventoryGui.cs:1799.

### [hud-build-8] Auga's build-menu tab paging (BuildMenuPaginationController / BuildHudTabFixer) never runs, and its tab-index == PieceCategory assumption is invalid now that PieceCategory has 9 entries driven by PieceTable.m_categories

- **Verdict:** partly (reviewer confidence 82)
- **Auga:** Auga/Hud_Setup.cs:145-149 (`var dummy = new GameObject("dummyBuildHud", ...); dummy.SetActive(false); var augaBuildHud = Object.Instantiate(Auga.Assets.BuildHud, dummy.transform);`) with AugaUnityLib/BuildMenuPaginationController.cs:63 and :110, AugaUnityLib/BuildHudTabFixer.cs:9-15
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Piece.PieceCategory (scratchpad/decomp/Piece.cs:53-66), PieceTable.m_categories / m_categoryLabels / m_selectedCategory (decomp/PieceTable.cs:13-15, 35), PieceTable.GetAvailablePiecesInCategory (decomp/PieceTable.cs:167-170), Hud.UpdateBuild (decomp/Hud.cs:1462-1482)
- **Trigger:** Any build session (the components are attached to a prefab instance parented to an inactive GameObject, so Unity never calls their Awake/Start/Update).
- **Consequence:** Auga ships no tab UI for 1.0's five new build categories, and the paging arrows/CategoryTitle that would have handled overflow are dead. If the prefab were ever re-activated, `RefreshVisibleObjects` would read the wrong counts and `ToggleBuildMenuTabs` would never find a match.
- **Evidence:** `dummy.SetActive(false)` (Hud_Setup.cs:147) executes before `Object.Instantiate(Auga.Assets.BuildHud, dummy.transform)` (line 149), so the instance is never activeInHierarchy and its MonoBehaviours never tick; the only other thing that block does with the prefab is read fonts off `DividerLarge/TabContainer/Tabs/Misc` (line 150). Two independent 1.0 breakages on top of that: (a) `BuildMenuPaginationController.cs:110` does `_player.m_buildPieces.GetAvailablePiecesInCategory((Piece.PieceCategory)i)` where `i` is an index into `Hud.m_pieceCategoryTabs`, but 1.0 drives tabs from the arbitrary list `public List<Piece.PieceCategory> m_categories` (decomp/PieceTable.cs:13) - `Hud.UpdateBuild` now reads `pieceTable.m_categories[i]` and `pieceTable.m_categoryLabels[i]` (decomp/Hud.cs:1470-1471) - so index and enum value are no longer the same thing, and `GetAvailablePiecesInCategory` silently clamps out-of-range categories with `Mathf.Min(m_availablePiecesByCategory.Count - 1, (int)cat)` (decomp/PieceTable.cs:169). (b) `BuildMenuPaginationController.cs:63` does `currentStartIndex = (int)_player.m_buildPieces.m_selectedCategory`, but 1.0 initialises that field to `Piece.PieceCategory.Max` = 9 (decomp/PieceTable.cs:35) rather than 0. Auga's BuildHud.prefab only carries four tab objects (Misc, Crafting, Building, Furniture per the m_Name list), while the enum grew to Misc/Crafting/BuildingWorkbench/BuildingStonecutter/Furniture/DeepNorth/Feasts/Food/Meads with Max = 9 (decomp/Piece.cs:53-66) and the vanilla tab strip now has six (dumps/Hud_vanilla.txt:114-137).
- **Fix:** Drive tabs off `PieceTable.m_categories` / `m_categoryLabels` rather than off array position: in BuildMenuPaginationController replace `(Piece.PieceCategory)i` with `pieceTable.m_categories[i]`, and compare against `m_buildPieces.GetSelectedCategory()` (which resolves the Max sentinel) instead of the raw `m_selectedCategory`. Related landmine to fix at the same time: `PieceTable.SetCategory(int index)` changed from `m_selectedCategory = (Piece.PieceCategory)Mathf.Clamp(index, 0, 3)` to an unchecked `m_selectedCategory = m_categories[index]` (decomp/PieceTable.cs:319-325), so Auga's still-exported helper `Hud_Setup.SetBuildCategory(int index)` (Auga/Hud_Setup.cs:279-285), which forwards a raw tab index to `Player.SetBuildCategory(int)`, will throw ArgumentOutOfRangeException the moment anything calls it with index >= m_categories.Count - it has no caller in the repo today, so use the `SetBuildCategory(Piece.PieceCategory)` overload 1.0 added instead.
- **Verifier correction:** The conclusion (Auga's paging never runs, and its index-as-PieceCategory assumption is invalid in 1.0) is right, but two supporting details are wrong. (1) BuildMenuPaginationController is not on the prefab that gets instantiated into the inactive dummy. It lives on HUD.prefab's `hudroot/BuildHud` (MonoBehaviour &1046502534777875257, script fileID 1099087801, guid 045bc81af5d75cb42a79645f3b1c6adb, HUD.prefab:17732-17751), and Auga never instantiates that branch at all — every use of Auga.Assets.Hud is a Replace/CopyOver of a specific hudroot/* path (HotKeyBar, StatusEffects, SaveIcon, BadConnectionIcon, LoadingBlack, EventBar, Damaged, crosshair, GuardianPower, FoodPanel0-2, HealthBar, StaminaBar, EitrBar, action_progress, staggerpanel, KeyHints, ShipHud, MiniMap/small, MiniMap/large) and BuildHud is not among them. BuildHud.prefab itself contains no maximumVisibleTabs/leftPageArrow keys; only BuildHudTabFixer (script -987503260, BuildHud.prefab:3392-3403) is on it, and for that component the inactive-dummy mechanism the finding describes is exactly correct. (2) 'Auga ships no tab UI for 1.0's five new build categories' is false — Auga keeps and restyles the LIVE vanilla tab strip: Hud_Setup.cs:160-190 iterates `m_pieceSelectionWindow.transform.Find("Categories")` children and copies Auga's fonts/colours onto each, and dumps/Hud_vanilla.txt:114-137 shows all six vanilla tabs (Misc, Crafting, Building, Building2, Furniture, Deep North) present. Auga's own four-tab BuildHud.prefab is used only as a font donor (Hud_Setup.cs:150) plus two Replace calls for Darken/Bkg2.
- **Verifier's better fix:** Fix is right in direction. Because the component is dead code, the first step is to decide whether to wire it up at all — if Auga keeps restyling the vanilla tab strip, BuildMenuPaginationController and BuildHudTabFixer should be deleted rather than fixed; if it is to be revived, it must be moved onto an object Auga actually instantiates before the index->category mapping matters.

### [harmony-7] Menu.UpdateNavigation transpiler re-implementation omits the 1.0 buttons (m_skipButton, m_inviteButton) and never sets Navigation.Mode.Explicit

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 82)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\PauseMenu_Setup.cs:119-234 (Menu_UpdateNavigation_Patch.UpdateNavigation + Transpiler that replaces the whole body)
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Menu.UpdateNavigation (decomp\Menu.cs) - rewritten in 1.0 to build the list from the serialized fields m_continueButton, m_skipButton, m_saveButton, m_playerListButton, m_inviteButton, m_settingsButton, m_logoutButton, m_quitButton
- **Trigger:** Open the pause menu with a gamepad and navigate up/down (once the SetButtonsEnabled crash above is fixed).
- **Consequence:** 'Skip intro' and the new 'Invite friends' entry are never in the gamepad navigation chain, and because Auga's replacement builds `navigation with { selectOnUp, selectOnDown }` without assigning `mode`, whichever Navigation.Mode the Auga prefab's buttons carry is kept - 1.0 forces Navigation.Mode.Explicit, so if the prefab says Automatic the explicit links are ignored entirely.
- **Evidence:** 1.0 body: `m_firstMenuButton = m_continueButton; list.Add(m_continueButton); if (m_skipButton.gameObject.activeSelf) list.Add(m_skipButton); if (m_saveButton.interactable) list.Add(m_saveButton); if (m_playerListButton.gameObject.activeSelf) list.Add(m_playerListButton); if (m_inviteButton.gameObject.activeSelf) list.Add(m_inviteButton); list.Add(m_settingsButton); list.Add(m_logoutButton); if (m_quitButton.gameObject.activeSelf) list.Add(m_quitButton); ... navigation.mode = Navigation.Mode.Explicit;`. Auga's replacement builds its list from `instance.m_menuDialog.Find("MenuEntries/Logout"|"MenuEntries/Exit"|"MenuEntries/DividerMedium/CloseButton"|"MenuEntries/Settings"|"MenuEntries/Compendium")` plus `instance.m_saveButton` / `instance.m_playerListButton`, and sets only selectOnUp/selectOnDown. The vanilla-branch paths it uses do still exist (dumps\Menu_vanilla.txt:21 Continue, :42 Settings, :46 Logout, :50 Exit) but `MenuEntries/SkipIntro` (:25) and `MenuEntries/InviteFriends` (:38) are never referenced.
- **Fix:** Add `m_skipButton` and `m_inviteButton` to both branches of the replacement list (guarded on gameObject.activeSelf as vanilla does) and set `navigation.mode = Navigation.Mode.Explicit` in the `with` expression; the Auga menu prefab also needs its own Invite Friends entry, otherwise the donor's hidden button is all that exists.
- **Verifier's better fix:** Fix is right. Two framing corrections for the write-up: SkipIntro was already in 0.217's list (decomp_old_assembly_valheim/Menu.cs:128, 132-135), so Auga's replacement dropped it back in 2023 — only m_inviteButton is genuinely new — and 0.217 also assigned the mode (`navigation.mode = (Hud.instance.IsVisible() || Player.m_localPlayer?.InIntro()) ? Explicit : None`, :169), so the missing mode is pre-existing debt rather than a 1.0 regression. Also note Show() calls SetButtonsEnabled at :228 before UpdateNavigation at :238, so this path stays unreachable from Show until the m_skipButton reference in AugaMenu.prefab is fixed.

### [hud-bars-3] Hud.m_gpTouchButton is left on the hidden GuardianPower_VanillaDonor; Auga's GuardianPower/Icon has no UIInputHandler, so 1.0's click/tap-to-activate guardian power is dead

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 80) - crash already prevented by PortCarryOver
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\Hud_Setup.cs:82-88 — `__instance.m_gpRoot = (RectTransform)__instance.Replace("hudroot/GuardianPower", Auga.Assets.Hud);` then reassigns m_gpName, m_gpIcon, m_gpCooldown only. AugaUnity/Assets/Prefabs/HUD.prefab: hudroot/GuardianPower children are GPBkg, Icon (CanvasRenderer + Image only), Name, GPTimeText, GPKey.
- **Vanilla 1.0:** Hud.m_gpTouchButton (decomp/Hud.cs:164); wired in Hud.Awake (`gpTouchButton.m_onLeftClick = Delegate.Combine(..., OnGuardianPowerClicked)`); handler Hud.OnGuardianPowerClicked (decomp/Hud.cs:1626) calls Player.StartGuardianPower()
- **Trigger:** Clicking / double-tapping the guardian-power icon in the HUD (the pointer path 1.0 added; the keyboard hotkey path is unaffected).
- **Consequence:** No exception — Hud.Awake runs before Auga's postfix so the delegate is hooked successfully, but it is hooked to the UIInputHandler inside the branch Auga then turns into an inactive donor. That object can no longer receive raycasts, and Auga's replacement icon carries no UIInputHandler/GuiButton, so the guardian power can never be activated by clicking or tapping it.
- **Evidence:** auga_audit/dumps/Hud_vanilla.txt:89 — `hudroot/GuardianPower/Icon  [Image, GuiButton, UIInputHandler]`; it is the only UIInputHandler anywhere under hudroot/GuardianPower (lines 87-94). Auga's parsed HUD.prefab shows `HUD/hudroot/GuardianPower/Icon   [u222,MBfe87c0]` — CanvasRenderer plus an Image (guid fe87c0...) and nothing else. m_gpTouchButton is a "NewIn10" field in auga_field_diff.csv and is absent from the `15 unassigned` PortDiagnostics list, confirming it is non-null and pointing at the donor. Extensions.cs:89 `donor = PortCarryOver.MakeDonor(foundOriginal.gameObject);` -> PortCarryOver.cs:25 `original.SetActive(false);`.
- **Fix:** In Hud_Setup.cs after line 84 add `__instance.m_gpTouchButton = __instance.m_gpIcon.gameObject.GetComponent<UIInputHandler>() ?? __instance.m_gpIcon.gameObject.AddComponent<UIInputHandler>();` and re-hook the handler (`m_gpTouchButton.m_onLeftClick += __instance.OnGuardianPowerClicked`), since vanilla Hud.Awake has already run by the time the postfix executes. Add the UIInputHandler to the Icon object in HUD.prefab for the permanent fix.

### [minimap-5] 1.0's new touch map buttons (small/MapClick -> OnSmallMapClick, large/OutsideMapClick -> OnOutsideMapClick) have no counterpart in Auga's minimap prefab

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 80)

- **Trigger:** On a touchscreen: tap the small minimap to open the large map, or tap outside the large map to close it.

Auga's parsed prefab tree has neither name anywhere.

### [main-menu-7] 1.0 FejdStartup has menu UI Auga's MainMenu prefab has no counterpart for, including two SceneReference fields PortCarryOver structurally cannot fill

- **Verdict:** confirmed (reviewer confidence 80)
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\MainMenu_Setup.cs:40 (`__instance.Replace("Menu", Auga.Assets.MainMenuPrefab)`) and the field assignments at 43-191; C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\PortCarryOver.cs:104-120
- **Vanilla 1.0:** FejdStartup fields and FejdStartup.Awake / SetupGui / HideAll (decomp/FejdStartup.cs:15-306, 307-372, 505-533)
- **Trigger:** Re-enabling the menu swap; then every menu screen that touches one of these.
- **Consequence:** Fields pointing into the replaced 'Menu' branch become destroyed references, and fields that are not UnityEngine.Object are invisible to both PortCarryOver and PortDiagnostics so they stay at their 2023 defaults with no warning. `HideAll()` alone dereferences `m_characterSelectScreen` and `m_cinematicsMenuList`, and `Awake()` dereferences `m_crossplayServerToggle`, `m_menuList`, `m_csSourceInfo`, `m_csFileSource` and `m_serverOptions` - all before any Auga postfix could repair them.
- **Evidence:** 1.0 fields with no assignment anywhere in MainMenu_Setup and no matching object in MainMenu.prefab's name table: m_characterSelectScreen, m_crossplayServerToggle, m_samePlatformOnlyToggleJoinPanel, m_samePlatformOnlyToggleWorldPanel, m_worldSourceInfo, m_worldSourceInfoPanel, m_tooltipAnchor, m_tooltipSecondaryAnchor, m_merchStoreButtonParent, m_showEulaButton, m_showChangelogButton, m_changeLogNotice, m_cinematicsMenuList, m_cinematicsEntry, m_cinematicsEntryLocked, m_cinematicsBack, m_manageSavesMenu, m_cloudStorageWarningNextSave, m_eulaWindow, m_serverOptionsButton, m_newVersionName, m_cameraMarkerSaves, m_moddedText. Two of them are non-Object: `public SceneReference m_startScene;` and `public SceneReference m_mainScene;` (decomp/FejdStartup.cs:99-101) - PortCarryOver.FillComponent only assigns when `typeof(Object).IsAssignableFrom(field.FieldType)` or `IsObjectCollection(...)`, so a SceneReference struct is skipped, and PortDiagnostics.Postfix likewise skips it (`if (!typeof(UnityEngine.Object).IsAssignableFrom(field.FieldType)) continue;`). Run4 already demonstrates this class of gap on the pause menu: `Menu on 'AugaMenu(Clone)': 11 empty references filled ...` does not list `m_startScene`.
- **Fix:** Before uncommenting the Replace("Menu", ...) call, build the missing objects into MainMenu.prefab (or point the fields at the hidden donor via PortCarryOver), and extend PortCarryOver.FillComponent with an explicit copy for non-Object serialized fields such as SceneReference so the menu/pause-menu scene links survive the swap.

### [world-ui-3] 1.0 radial-menu key hints are stranded in the hidden KeyHints donor, so the radial hint strip is permanently blank

- **Verdict:** partly (reviewer confidence 80) - crash already prevented by PortCarryOver
- **Auga:** C:\Users\user_\Documents\GitHub\Valheim_Creative\Auga\Auga\Hud_Setup.cs:201 `var keyHints = __instance.transform.Replace("hudroot/KeyHints", Auga.Assets.Hud);`; AugaUnity\Assets\Prefabs\HUD.prefab, KeyHints component on GO "KeyHints": `m_radialHints: {fileID: 6032538681940285978}`, `m_radialBackHint: {fileID: 0}`, `m_radialInteract: {fileID: 0}`, `m_radialBack: {fileID: 0}`, and no m_radialKeyHints key at all
- **Vanilla 1.0:** KeyHints.UpdateHints (decomp\KeyHints.cs:195-204 and :301-304) and KeyHints.SetGamePadBindings (decomp\KeyHints.cs:109); KeyHintsRadial.UpdateRadialHints (decomp\KeyHintsRadial.cs:71)
- **Trigger:** Opening the 1.0 radial menu (gamepad radial / OpenEmote), which makes Hud.instance.m_radialMenu.Active true.
- **Consequence:** KeyHints does `m_radialHints.SetActive(value: true)` on Auga's own RadialHints object, whose three 2023 hint fields are all {fileID: 0}, so nothing is shown. The real 1.0 hints (interact / back / close / closeTopLevel / drop / drop-multiple, keyboard and gamepad variants) live on the donor's KeyHints/RadialHints object that PortCarryOver pointed m_radialKeyHints at, but that object is inside the inactive KeyHints_VanillaDonor, so `if (m_radialKeyHints.isActiveAndEnabled)` at KeyHints.cs:301 is always false and UpdateRadialHints(Hud.instance.m_radialMenu) never runs. Net: the radial menu has no key hints at all under Auga.
